Walters, of course, would be identified with the label ‘butler,’ and I can’t imagine Rivers sitting down and drinking with a ‘butler.’ He would only drink with people whom he thought of as his equals, that is, people whom he identified with class-labels of equal social importance to his own labels of ‘antiquarian’ and ‘businessman.’ ”

===Chinese===
「那得看情形。我得先看看弗莱明家的手枪，有没有哪支在里弗斯的遗物里冒出来。就我个人而言，我差不多已经认定，杀他的就是同他对饮的那个人。没有任何迹象表明事后还有旁人进过那家店。倘若如此，凶手是不是沃尔特斯，我倒犯嘀咕。你知道里弗斯这人势利成什么样子。而就我对他的了解，他看人彻头彻尾是亚里士多德的一套：把一个个活人等同于类别的标签。沃尔特斯呢，自然顶的是『管家』这个标签；而里弗斯坐下来同一位『管家』对酌——这样的场面我想象不出。他只肯同他视为对等的人喝酒——也就是说，同那些在他眼里所贴标签的社会分量，与他自属的『古董商』『生意人』两个标签分量相当的人喝酒。」

===Original===
“That sounds like Korzybski,” Pierre said, as they turned onto Route 19 in the village and headed east. “You’ve read *Science and Sanity*?”

===Chinese===
「这一套听着像科尔兹布斯基的口气，」皮埃尔说，这时车子在村子里拐上 19 号公路，朝东而去。「《科学与心智健全》你读过？」

===Original===
Rand nodded. “Yes. I first read it in the 1933 edition, back about 1936; I’ve been rereading it every couple of years since. The principles of General Semantics come in very handy in my business, especially in criminal-investigation work, like this. A consciousness of abstracting, a realization that we can only know something about a thin film of events on the surface of any given situation, and a habit of thinking structurally and of individual things, instead of verbally and of categories, saves a lot of blind-alley chasing. And they suggest a great many more avenues of investigation than would be evident to one whose thinking is limited by intensional, verbal, categories.”

===Chinese===
兰德点了点头。「读过。头一回读的是 1933 年版，大约 1936 年那阵；打那以后，每隔两三年就重读一遍。普通语义学（General Semantics）的种种原理，在我这一行里用起来顺手得很，尤其在刑事调查工作里，比如眼下这桩。对抽象过程保有一份自觉，明白我们对任何局面的了解，都只能及于表层薄薄一层事件，再养成按结构、按个体事物思考的习惯，而不囿于字眼和门类——这就省去了许多钻死胡同的冤枉工夫。而且比起思维被内涵的、字面的门类框死的人，它们还能提示出多得多的调查门路。」

===Original===
“Yes. I find General Semantics helpful in my work, too,” Pierre said. “I can use it in plotting a story.﻿ … Oh-oh!”

===Chinese===
「是啊。我干我这行，也觉得普通语义学帮得上忙，」皮埃尔说，「编故事的布局时用得上。……哎呀不好！」

===Original===
“The Gentlemen of the Press,” Rand said, looking ahead as the car approached the Rivers house and shop. “There hasn’t been a good, sensational, murder story for some time; this is a gift from the gods.”

===Chinese===
「报界诸公到了，」兰德说，一面望向前方，车子正驶近里弗斯的住宅和店铺。「有些日子没出过像样的、轰动的凶杀新闻了；这可是老天爷白送的一份厚礼。」

===Original===
A swarm of cars were parked in front and beside the redbrick house. Among them, Rand spotted a gold-lettered green sedan of the New Belfast *Dispatch* and *Evening Express*, a black coupé bearing the blazonry of the New Belfast *Mercury*, cars from a couple of papers at Louisburg, the state capital, and cars from papers as far distant as Pittsburgh, Buffalo, and Cincinnati. In front of the shop, a motley assemblage of journalists was interviewing and photographing an undersized runt in a tan Chesterfield topcoat and a gray Homburg hat, whom they were addressing as Mr. Farnsworth. The District Attorney of Scott County had a mustache which failed miserably to make him look like Tom Dewey; he impressed Rand as the sort of offensive little squirt who compensates for his general insignificance by bad manners and loud-mouthed self-assertion. Corporal Kavaalen, standing in the doorway of the shop, caught sight of Rand and his companion as they got out of the car and came to meet them, hustling them around the crowd and into the shop before anybody could notice and recognize them.

===Chinese===
红砖宅子的房前屋侧，停了一大片汽车。其中兰德认出了《新贝尔法斯特电讯暨晚快报》一辆描着金字的绿轿车、《新贝尔法斯特水星报》一辆涂着报徽的黑色双门轿车，州府路易斯堡两家报馆的车，还有远自匹兹堡、布法罗、辛辛那提赶来的几家报纸的车。店门前，一群五色杂陈的记者，正围着一个穿驼色切斯特菲尔德式长大衣、戴灰色洪堡帽的矮小家伙采访、拍照，口口声声「法恩斯沃思先生」。这位斯科特县地区检察官唇上蓄着一撇胡子，指望沾出几分汤姆·杜威的派头，却败得惨不忍睹；他给兰德的印象，是那种浑身找不出一点分量、便靠出言无状和大嗓门的自命不凡来撑场面的讨嫌小个儿。卡瓦伦下士站在店门口，瞧见兰德和他的同伴下车，便迎上前来，赶在旁人察觉认出之前，领着两人绕开人群进了店。

===Original===
“That was a good tip, about the telephone,” he said softly. “Mick checked at the Rosemont exchange. Rivers got a long-distance call from Topeka last night; ten fifteen to ten seventeen. We got the night long distance operator out of bed, and she confirmed it; Rivers took the call himself. He gets a lot of long distance calls in the evenings; she knew his voice.” He corrected himself, shifting to the past tense and glancing, as he did, at the chalk outline on the floor, now scuffed by many feet, and the dried bloodstains. “You say this puts Gresham in the clear?”

===Chinese===
「电话那条线索，提得好，」他轻声说。「米克去罗斯蒙特的电话交换台查过了。昨晚里弗斯接了一个托皮卡打来的长途；十点十五到十点十七。我们把夜班长途接线员从被窝里挖了起来，她证实了；电话是里弗斯亲自接的。他晚上常接好多长途；她听得出他的嗓音。」他随即自行更正，改用了过去时态，一边改，一边瞟了一眼地上那圈粉笔轮廓——已被无数双脚蹭得残缺——和那摊干了的血迹。「你是说，这一来格雷沙姆就洗清了？」

===Original===
“Absolutely,” Rand assured him. “He was at home from nine twenty-two on.” He introduced Pierre Jarrett, and explained their mission. “You find anything except what’s here in the shop?”

===Chinese===
「板上钉钉，」兰德向他打包票，「从九点二十二分起，他就在家里了。」他引见了皮埃尔·贾勒特，说明了两人的来意。「除了这店里的东西，还搜出什么别的没有？」

===Original===
“Only Rivers’s own .38 Smith & Wesson, in his room, and a lot of pistols out in the garage, that look like junk to me,” Kavaalen said. “I’ll show them to you.”

===Chinese===
「只有里弗斯自己那支 .38 口径史密斯-韦森，在他房间里；再就是车库里一大堆手枪，依我看全是破烂，」卡瓦伦说。「我带你们去瞧瞧。」

===Original===
Rand nodded. “Pierre, you look around the shop; I’ll see what this other stuff is.”

===Chinese===
兰德点了点头。「皮埃尔，你在店里四处看看；我去瞧瞧那批东西是什么货色。」

===Original===
He followed Kavaalen through a door at the rear of the shop; the same one through which Cecil Gillis had carried the Kentucky rifle the afternoon before. Beside Rivers’s car, there was a long workbench in the garage, and piles of wood and cardboard cartons, and stacks of newspapers, and a barrel full of excelsior, all evidently used in preparing arms for shipment. There was also a large pile of old pistols, and a number of long-arms.

===Chinese===
他跟着卡瓦伦穿过店铺后部的一扇门——正是前一天下午塞西尔·吉利斯抱着那支肯塔基步枪走出去的那一扇。车库里，里弗斯的汽车旁边摆着一张长工作台，还有成堆的木料和纸箱、成摞的报纸、满满一桶刨花，显然都是打包发运枪械用的。另有一大堆旧手枪，还有几支长枪。

===Original===
Rand pawed among the pistols; they were, as the State Police corporal had said, all junk. The sort of things a dealer has to buy, at times, in order to get something really good. Many of them had been partially dismantled for parts. When he was certain that the heap of junk-weapons didn’t conceal anything of value, he returned to the shop. Pierre was waiting for him by Rivers’s desk.

===Chinese===
兰德在那堆手枪里翻拣了一阵；正如那位州警下士所言，全是破烂。都是经销商为了搭到真正的好货、有时不得不整批吃进的那类东西。其中不少已被拆得七零八落，取走了零件。等他确信这堆废枪底下没藏着什么值钱货，便回到店里。皮埃尔正在里弗斯的写字台旁等他。

===Original===
He shook his head. “Not a thing,” he reported. “I found a couple of out-and-out fakes, and about ten or fifteen that had been altered in one way or another, and a lot of reblued stuff, but nothing from Fleming’s collection. What did you find?”

===Chinese===
皮埃尔摇了摇头。「一件也没有，」他汇报道，「我找出两件彻头彻尾的假货，约莫十件到十五件这样那样动过手脚的，还有一大批重新烤蓝的货色，可弗莱明收藏里的东西，一件都没有。你那边查出什么了？」

===Original===
Rand laughed. “I found Rivers’s scrapheap, and some pistols that probably contributed parts to some of the stuff you found,” he said. “Of course, all we can say is that the stuff isn’t here; Rivers could have bought it, and stored it outside somewhere. But even so, I’m not taking the Fleming butler too seriously as a suspect for the murder.”

===Chinese===
兰德笑了。「我找到的是里弗斯的废品堆，还有一批手枪，多半给你翻出来的那些东西供过零件，」他说。「当然，眼下只能说这批货不在这店里；里弗斯也可能买下了，存在外头什么地方。不过即便如此，弗莱明家的管家作为凶案嫌疑人，我是不打算太当真的。」

===Original===
“What’s this about Fleming’s butler?” a voice broke in. “Have you been withholding information from me?”

===Chinese===
「弗莱明家的管家是怎么回事？」一个声音插了进来。「你一直对我隐瞒线索？」

===Original===
Rand turned, to find that Farnsworth had left the press conference in front and crepe-soled up on him from behind.

===Chinese===
兰德转过身，发现法恩斯沃思已撇下门前的记者招待会，踩着那双绉胶底鞋，悄没声儿地从他背后蹭了过来。

===Original===
“I withheld a theory, which seems to have come to nothing,” he replied.

===Chinese===
「我瞒下的是一套推想，如今看是要落空了，」他答道。

===Original===
Kavaalen told the D.A. who Rand was. “He’s cooperating with us,” he added. “Sergeant McKenna instructed us to give him every consideration.”

===Chinese===
卡瓦伦告诉地区检察官兰德是什么人。「他在同我们合作，」他补了一句，「麦肯纳中士吩咐过，对他要事事行个方便。」

===Original===
“It seems that a number of valuable pistols were stolen from the collection of the late Lane Fleming,” Rand said. “We suspected that the butler had stolen them and sold them to Rivers; I thought it possible that he might also have killed Rivers to silence him about the transaction.” He shrugged. “None of the stolen items have turned up here, so there’s nothing to connect the thefts with the death of Rivers.”

===Chinese===
「已故莱恩·弗莱明的收藏里，看来被偷走了若干贵重手枪，」兰德说。「我们疑心是管家偷了卖给里弗斯；我想他也可能索性杀了里弗斯灭口，好把这笔买卖封死。」他耸了耸肩。「被盗的东西一件也没在这儿冒头，所以失窃和里弗斯之死之间，眼下还连不上线。」

===Original===
“Good heavens, you certainly didn’t suspect a prominent and respected citizen like Mr. Rivers of receiving stolen goods?” Farnsworth demanded, aghast.

===Chinese===
「老天爷，你总不会怀疑里弗斯先生这样一位显赫、受人敬重的公民收赃吧？」法恩斯沃思大惊失色地质问。

===Original===
“Who respects him?” Rand hooted. “Rivers was a notorious swindler; he had that reputation among arms-collectors all over the country. He was expelled from membership in the National Rifle Association for misrepresentation and fraud. Why, he even swindled Lane Fleming on a pair of fake pistols, a week or so before Fleming’s death. And the very reason why your man Olsen was inclined to suspect Stephen Gresham was that he had had trouble with Rivers about a crooked deal Rivers had put over on him. Fortunately, Mr. Gresham has since been cleared of any suspicion, but﻿—”

===Chinese===
「谁敬重他？」兰德嗤笑一声。「里弗斯是个臭名昭著的骗子；在全国的枪械收藏家堆里，他这名声早就传遍了。他因为虚饰假冒、欺诈行骗，被全国步枪协会除了名。嗐，就连莱恩·弗莱明，也在去世前个把星期，叫他用一对假枪坑过。而你那位奥尔森之所以倾向于疑心斯蒂芬·格雷沙姆，恰恰是因为格雷沙姆吃过里弗斯的一记阴招、为这桩黑买卖同他闹过纠纷。所幸格雷沙姆先生如今已洗脱了一切嫌疑，不过——」

===Original===
“Who says he’s been cleared?” Farnsworth snapped. “He’s still a suspect.”

===Chinese===
「谁说他洗脱了？」法恩斯沃思厉声道。「他仍是嫌疑人。」

===Original===
“Sergeant McKenna says so,” Corporal Kavaalen declared. “He has been cleared. I guess we just didn’t get around to telling you about that.” He went on to explain about the long distance call that had furnished Stephen Gresham’s alibi.

===Chinese===
「麦肯纳中士说的，」卡瓦伦下士朗声道。「他已经洗清了。大概是我们一直没腾出工夫，把这一桩知会你。」接着，他把那通为斯蒂芬·格雷沙姆提供不在场证明的长途电话解释了一遍。

===Original===
“And Gresham was at home from nine twenty-two on,” Rand added. “There are eight witnesses to that: His wife and daughter; myself; Captain Jarrett, here; and his fiancée, Miss Lawrence; Philip Cabot; Adam Trehearne; Colin MacBride.”

===Chinese===
「而且从九点二十二分起，格雷沙姆就在家里，」兰德补充道，「有八位证人：他的妻子和女儿；我本人；这边的贾勒特上尉，还有他的未婚妻劳伦斯小姐；菲利普·卡伯特；亚当·特雷赫恩；科林·麦克布赖德。」

===Original===
Farnsworth looked bewildered. “Why wasn’t I told about that?” he demanded sulkily.

===Chinese===
法恩斯沃思一脸茫然。「这事怎么没人告诉我？」他怏怏地责问。

===Original===
“Sergeant McKenna’s been too busy, and I didn’t think of it,” Kavaalen said insolently. “I’m not supposed to report to you, anyhow. Why didn’t your man Olsen tell you; he was with us when we checked with the telephone company.”

===Chinese===
「麦肯纳中士忙得脚不沾地，我呢，又没想起来，」卡瓦伦答得没好气。「再说，本来也没派我向你汇报。你那位奥尔森怎么不告诉你？我们同电话公司核对的时候，他可是在场的。」

===Original===
Farnsworth tried to ignore that by questioning Pierre about the time of Gresham’s arrival home, then turned to Rand and wanted to know what the latter’s interest in the case was.

===Chinese===
法恩斯沃思竭力装作没听见，转头盘问皮埃尔，格雷沙姆到家是在几点几分，随后又转向兰德，想知道后者在这案子里算是什么干系。

===Original===
Rand told him about his work in connection with the Fleming collection, producing Humphrey Goode’s letter of authorization. Farnsworth seemed impressed in about the same way as the coroner, Kirchner, but he was still puzzled.

===Chinese===
兰德讲了讲他经手弗莱明收藏的这摊差事，出示了汉弗莱·古德的授权信。法恩斯沃思看上去像验尸官基希纳那样被唬住了几分，可仍旧一头雾水。

===Original===
“But I understood that you had been retained by Stephen Gresham, to investigate this murder,” he said.

===Chinese===
「可是我听说的，却是斯蒂芬·格雷沙姆聘了你，调查这桩凶案，」他说。

===Original===
“So you did talk to Olsen, after I saw him,” Rand pounced. “Odd he didn’t mention this telephone thing.﻿ … Why, yes; that’s true. My agency handles all sorts of business. The two operations aren’t mutually exclusive; for a while, I even thought they might be related, but now﻿—” He shrugged.

===Chinese===
「这么说，我见过奥尔森之后，你到底还是同他谈过了，」兰德一把抓住话头。「怪的是，他没提这桩电话的事。……哦，没错；确有其事。敝社什么生意都接。两摊事由并不互相排斥；有一阵我甚至疑心它们或许相互牵连，可如今——」他耸了耸肩。

===Original===
“Well, you believe, now, that Rivers had nothing to do with the pistols you say were stolen from the Fleming collection?” Farnsworth asked. Rand shook his head ambiguously; Farnsworth took that for a negative answer to his question, as he was intended to. “And you say Mr. Gresham has been completely cleared of any suspicion of complicity in this murder?”

===Chinese===
「那么，你现在认为，里弗斯同你所说的、从弗莱明收藏里偷走的那批手枪无关了？」法恩斯沃思问。兰德模棱两可地摇了摇头；法恩斯沃思把这当成了对问题的否定回答——兰德要的正是这个效果。「你还说，格雷沙姆先生已彻底洗脱了参与本凶案的一切嫌疑？」

===Original===
“Mr. Rand’s helping us; we want him to stick around till the case is closed,” Corporal Kavaalen threw in, perceiving the drift of Farnsworth’s questions. “He and Sergeant McKenna have worked together before; he’s given us a lot of good tips.”

===Chinese===
「兰德先生在帮我们的忙；结案之前，我们要他留在本地随时效力，」卡瓦伦下士插话进来，他已听出法恩斯沃思这一串问话的走向。「他同麦肯纳中士从前就共过事；给我们出过不少好点子。」

===Original===
“You understand,” Rand took over, “Mr. Gresham didn’t retain me merely to help him clear himself. I don’t accept that kind of retainers. I was retained to find the murderer of Arnold Rivers, and I intend to continue working on this case until I do. I hope that the same friendly spirit of mutual cooperation will exist between your office and my agency as exists between me and the State Police. I certainly don’t want to have to work at cross purposes with any of the regular law-enforcement agencies.”

===Chinese===
「你要明白，」兰德接过话头，「格雷沙姆先生聘我，可不单是为了帮他洗清自己。这一类聘请，我是不接的。我受聘是要查出杀害阿诺德·里弗斯的凶手，我也打算把这案子一直办下去，直到查出来为止。我希望贵署同敝社之间，也能存在我同州警之间这种友好合作的精神。我当然不想同任何正规执法机关背道而驰、互相拆台。」

===Original===
“Oh, certainly; of course.” Farnsworth didn’t seem to like the idea, but there was no apparent opening for objection. He and Rand exchanged mendacious compliments, pledged close cooperation, and did practically everything but draw up and sign a treaty of alliance. Then Farnsworth and Corporal Kavaalen accompanied Rand and Pierre Jarrett to the front door.

===Chinese===
「哦，那是自然；当然当然。」法恩斯沃思似乎并不乐意，却又寻不出明显的破绽可供反对。他同兰德互道了一通口是心非的恭维，誓保密切合作，几乎就差起草并签署一份同盟条约了。随后，法恩斯沃思和卡瓦伦下士把兰德和皮埃尔·贾勒特送到店门口。

===Original===
Some of the reporters who were ravening outside must have spotted Rand as he had entered; they were all waiting for him to come out, and set up a monstrous ululation when he appeared in the doorway. With Farnsworth beaming approval, Rand assured the Press that he was no more than a mere spectator, that the State Police and the efficient District Attorney of Scott County had the situation well in hand, and that an arrest was expected within a matter of hours. Then he and Pierre hurried to his car and drove away.

===Chinese===
在外头饿鹰般转悠的记者中，想必有人瞧见兰德进去了；这会儿全都守着等他出来，他刚在门口一露面，便掀起一阵鬼哭狼嚎也似的喧响。法恩斯沃思在旁满面春风地赞许着，兰德向报界保证：自己不过是个看热闹的旁观者；州警同能干的斯科特县地区检察官已将局面稳稳掌控；数小时之内可望有人落网。随后他和皮埃尔匆匆上了车，驶离而去。

## XIV / 十四

===Original===
Neither of them spoke for a moment or two. Then, after they had left the criminological-journalistic uproar at the Rivers place behind and were approaching the village of Rosemont, Pierre turned to Rand.

===Chinese===
两人有一阵子谁也没作声。等把里弗斯宅子前犯罪学界加新闻界合伙闹出的那片喧嚣甩在身后、车子快到罗斯蒙特村时，皮埃尔转向兰德。

===Original===
“You know,” he said, “for a disciple of Korzybski, you came pretty close to confusing orders of abstraction, a couple of times, back there. You showed that Stephen was at home while Rivers was taking that phone call, a little after ten. But when you talk about clearing him completely, aren’t you overlooking the possibility that he came back to Rivers’s after you and Philip Cabot left the Gresham place?”

===Chinese===
「你可知道，」他说，「作为科尔兹布斯基的门徒，方才在那边，你有好几处险些就把抽象阶次搅混了。你证明的是：十点过一点儿、里弗斯接那通电话的当口，斯蒂芬人在家里。可你张口就说把他完全洗清了——这不正漏看了一种可能么：你和菲利普·卡伯特离开格雷沙姆家之后，他又折回了里弗斯店里？」

===Original===
Rand eased the foot-pressure on the gas and spared young Jarrett a side-glance before returning his attention to the road ahead.

===Chinese===
兰德松了松踩着油门的脚，分神侧瞥了年轻的贾勒特一眼，随即把注意力收回到前方的路上。

===Original===
“Understand,” Pierre hastened to add, “I don’t believe that Stephen was fool enough to kill Rivers over that fake North & Cheney, but weren’t you producing inferences that hadn’t been abstracted from any descriptive data?”

===Chinese===
「你可明白，」皮埃尔忙不迭补充，「我并不认为斯蒂芬会蠢到为那支假诺斯与切尼去杀里弗斯；可你那会儿推出来的，岂不都是些并非从任何描述性数据里抽象出来的推断？」

===Original===
“Pierre, when I’m working on a case like this, any resemblance between my opinions and the statements I may make is purely due to conscious considerations of policy,” Rand told him. “I don’t want Farnsworth or Mick McKenna going around bitching this operation up for me. If they feel justified in eliminating Gresham on the strength of that phone call, I’m satisfied, regardless of the semantics involved. Right now, the thing that’s worrying me is the ease with which I seem to have talked Farnsworth into laying off Gresham. He and Olsen both have single-track minds. They may just dismiss that telephone alibi, such as it is, as mere error of the mortal mind, and go right ahead building some kind of a ramshackle case against Gresham. Since they picked him for their entry, they won’t want to have to scratch him.﻿ ﻿… Damn, I wish I could think of where Walters could have sold those pistols!”

===Chinese===
「皮埃尔，我在办这类案子的时候，我的见解与我可能发表的言论之间但凡有一点相似，纯是出于策略上的自觉考量，」兰德告诉他，「我不愿意法恩斯沃思或是米克·麦肯纳四处插手，把我这盘行动搅得一团糟。他们要是凭那通电话便觉得有理由把格雷沙姆排除出局，我就满意了，语义上站不站得住，一概不论。这会儿真正叫我犯愁的是：我这回劝说法恩斯沃思放过格雷沙姆，未免太顺当了。他跟奥尔森两个都是一根筋的脑子。他们大可以把那套电话不在场证明——它满打满算也就那么点分量——只当作凡人头脑难免出的差错，往旁边一撂，接着去给格雷沙姆搭那种七拼八凑的案子。既然把他选作了报名出赛的马，他们就不乐意非得把他的名字划掉不可。……妈的，我要是能想起沃尔特斯会把那些手枪卖到哪儿去就好了！」

===Original===
“Well, if Rivers wasn’t involved somehow, why was he killed?” Pierre wondered. “Hey! Maybe Walters sold the pistols to Umholtz! He’s just as big a crook as Rivers was, only not quite so smart.”

===Chinese===
「那么，里弗斯要是横竖没沾上干系，他怎么会送命？」皮埃尔纳闷道，「嘿！兴许沃尔特斯把手枪卖给乌姆霍尔茨了！那家伙跟里弗斯一样，是个不折不扣的大骗子，只是脑瓜没他那么灵光。」

===Original===
Rand nodded thoughtfully. “Maybe so. And suppose Rivers found out about it, and tried to declare himself in on it. That stuff would be worth at least ten thousand; I doubt if whoever bought it paid Walters more than two. In the Umholtz-Rivers income bracket, the difference might be worth killing for.”

===Chinese===
兰德若有所思地点头。「也许是这样。再假设里弗斯探得了这层，想要亮明身份、硬插一股。那批东西少说值一万块；我怀疑不管买主是谁，付给沃尔特斯的顶多两千。搁在乌姆霍尔茨和里弗斯那档收入上，这笔差额就值得为它杀人了。」

===Original===
“That’s right. And Umholtz was in the infantry, in the other war; he served in the Twenty-eighth Division. He was trained to use a bayonet. And he’d pick that short Mauser; it has about the same weight and balance as a 1903 Springfield.”

===Chinese===
「这话在理。乌姆霍尔茨上一场大战是在步兵里当差，服的是第二十八师的役，受过使刺刀的训练。而且他会挑那支短毛瑟；那枪的分量和配重，跟 1903 型斯普林菲尔德大致相仿。」

===Original===
“Well, you know, the killer wouldn’t need to have been trained to use a bayonet,” Rand pointed out. “Mick McKenna made that point, this afternoon. There have been a lot of war-movies that showed bayonet fighting; pretty nearly everybody knows about the technique that was used. And against an unarmed and probably unsuspecting victim like Rivers, a great deal of proficiency wouldn’t be needed.” He slowed the car. “Up this road?” he asked.

===Chinese===
「话虽如此，你可知道，凶手未必需要受过使刺刀的训练，」兰德指出，「米克·麦肯纳今天下午就点过这一层。表现拼刺刀的战争片多如牛毛；那路手法，差不多人人都见识过。何况对付里弗斯这样手无寸铁、多半还毫无戒备的对象，也用不着多大的手上功夫。」他放慢车速。「走这条道？」他问。

===Original===
“Yes. That’s my place, over there.”

===Chinese===
「对。那边就是敝宅。」

===Original===
Pierre pointed to a white-walled, red-roofed house that lay against a hillside, about a mile ahead, making a vivid spot in the dull grays and greens of the early April landscape. It consisted of a square two-story block, with one-story wings projecting to give it an L-shaped floorplan. It reminded Rand of farmhouses he had seen in Sicily during the War.

===Chinese===
皮埃尔指向前面约一英里外一座傍着山坡的白墙红顶的房子；四月之初的原野灰绿暗淡，它正好点上一抹鲜亮。宅子是一座方正的两层主体，探出单层的两翼厢房，平面摆成一个 L 形。它叫兰德想起大战期间在西西里见过的那些农舍。

===Original===
“Come on in and see my stuff, if you have time,” Pierre invited, as Rand pulled to a stop in the driveway. “I think I told you what I collect﻿—personal combat arms, both firearms and edge-weapons.”

===Chinese===
「有工夫就进来瞧瞧我的藏品，」皮埃尔相邀，这时兰德把车停进宅前车道，「记得我同你讲过我收藏的是什么——个人格斗兵器，火器与利刃兼收。」

===Original===
They entered the front door, which opened directly into a large parlor, a brightly colored, cheerful room. A woman rose from a chair where she had been reading. She was somewhere between forty-five and fifty, but her figure was still trim, and she retained much of what, in her youth, must have been great beauty.

===Chinese===
两人进了前门，门直通一间宽大的客厅，屋里色彩明快，一派融融喜气。一位妇人从正坐着看书的椅子里起身。她约在四十五到五十之间，身段依旧利落；年轻时想必有过惊人的美貌，如今也还留存大半。

===Original===
“Mother, this is Colonel Rand,” Pierre said. “Jeff, my mother.”

===Chinese===
「母亲，这位是兰德上校，」皮埃尔说，「杰夫，这是我母亲。」

===Original===
Rand shook hands with her, and said something polite. She gave him a smile of real pleasure.

===Chinese===
兰德同她握了手，讲了几句客套话。她给他一个由衷欢喜的微笑。

===Original===
“Pierre has been telling me about you, Colonel,” she said. There was a faint trace of French accent in her voice. “I suppose he brought you here to show you his treasures?”

===Chinese===
「皮埃尔一直在同我讲您的事，上校，」她说。她的嗓音里带着一缕淡淡的法国腔。「我猜，他领您上家里来，是要给您看看他的宝贝吧？」

===Original===
“Yes; I collect arms too. Pistols,” Rand said.

===Chinese===
「正是；我也收藏兵器。手枪，」兰德说。

===Original===
She laughed. “You gun-collectors; you’re like women looking at somebody’s new hat.﻿ ﻿… Will you stay for dinner with us, Colonel Rand?”

===Chinese===
她笑出声来。「你们这些玩枪的；就跟女人们凑在一处端详人家的新帽子一个模样。……留下来同我们吃晚饭吧，兰德上校？」

===Original===
“Why, I’m sorry; I can’t. I have a great many things to do, and I’m expected for dinner at the Flemings’. I really wish I could, Mrs. Jarrett. Maybe some other time.”

===Chinese===
「哎呀，真不巧，不成。我手头有一大摊子事要办，弗莱明家的晚饭也等着我去吃。我实在愿意留下，贾勒特太太。只好看改日了。」

===Original===
They chatted for a few minutes, then Pierre guided Rand into one of the wings of the house.

===Chinese===
几人闲谈了几分钟，随后皮埃尔引着兰德进了宅子一侧的厢房。

===Original===
“This is my workshop, too,” he said. “Here’s where I do my writing.” He opened a door and showed Rand into a large room.

===Chinese===
「这里也是我的工房，」他说，「我写东西就在这间。」他推开一扇门，让兰德进了一间大屋。

===Original===
On one side, the wall was blank; on the other, it was pierced by two small casement windows. The far end was of windows for its entire width, from within three feet of the floor almost to the ceiling. There were bookcases on either long side, and on the rear end, and over them hung Pierre’s weapons. Rand went slowly around the room, taking everything in. Very few of the arms were of issue military type, and most of these showed alterations to suit individual requirements. As Pierre had told him the evening before, the emphasis was upon weapons which illustrated techniques of combat.

===Chinese===
一侧的墙面空无一物；另一侧开着一对小小的平开窗。屋子尽头则整面皆是窗，从离地不到三英尺处几乎直抵天花板。两条长墙和末端墙上都立着书柜，柜子上方便悬着皮埃尔的兵器。兰德绕着屋子缓缓走了一遭，把样样都收进眼底。兵器里少有制式军用型号，有数的几件也多半经了改造，以合个人所需。正如皮埃尔前一晚说过的那样，收藏的重心，落在那些演示格斗技艺的兵器上。

===Original===
At the end of the room, lighted by the wide windows, was a long desk which was really a writer’s assembly line, with typewriter, reference-books, stacks of notes and manuscripts, and a big dictionary on a stand beside a comfortable swivel-chair.

===Chinese===
屋子尽头，借着那一整面大窗的光，是一张长书桌——实则是一条作家的流水线：打字机、参考书、一摞摞笔记与手稿，一部大词典立在旁侧的架上，配着一把舒舒服服的转椅。

===Original===
“What are you writing?” Rand asked.

===Chinese===
「你在写什么？」兰德问。

===Original===
“Science-fiction. I do a lot of stories for the pulps,” Pierre told him. “*Space-Trails*, and *Other Worlds*, and *Wonder-Stories*; mags like that. Most of it’s standardized formula-stuff; what’s known to the trade as space-operas. My best stuff goes to *Astonishing*. Parenthetically, you mustn’t judge any of these magazines by their names. It seems to be a convention to use hyperbolic names for science-fiction magazines; a heritage from what might be called an earlier and ruder day. What I do for *Astonishing* is really hard work, and I enjoy it. I’m working now on one for them, based on J. W. Dunne’s time-theories, if you know what they are.”

===Chinese===
「科幻小说。我给通俗杂志（pulp）写了好多故事，」皮埃尔告诉他，「《太空航迹》（Space-Trails）啦，《异世界》（Other Worlds）啦，《惊奇故事》（Wonder-Stories）啦，都是那一类刊物。多半是照标准配方出来的货色；行里人管它叫『太空歌剧』。我最拿得出手的东西，投给《惊人故事》（Astonishing）。附带说一句，这些杂志你可千万别凭刊名下判断。给科幻杂志起夸张的名号，似乎已成行规；那是不妨称之为较早而粗陋的年代传下的遗产。我给《惊人故事》干的才是真功夫，我也写得快活。眼下我正在给他们赶一篇，以 J. W. 邓恩（J. W. Dunne）的时间理论做底子——你可知道那是些什么？」

===Original===
“I think so,” Rand said. “Polydimensional time, isn’t it? Based on an effect Dunne observed and described﻿—dreams obviously related to some waking event, but preceding rather than following the event to which they are related. I read Dunne’s *Science and Psychical Phenomena* some years before the war, and once, when I had nothing better to do, I recorded dreams for about a month. I got a few doubtful-to-fair examples, and two unmistakable Dunne-Effect dreams. I never got anything that would help me pick a race-winner or spot a rise in the stock market, though.”

===Chinese===
「我想我知道，」兰德说，「多维时间，是不是？依据的是邓恩观察到并描述过的一种效应——梦明显关联着某件醒时的事，可它赶在那件事之先，而非缀在其后。战前好些年，我读过邓恩的《科学与心灵现象》（Science and Psychical Phenomena）；有一回闲着无事，我把自己的梦记了大约一个月。得到几例半信半疑、勉强及格的样本，还有两个错不了的邓恩效应之梦。只是我从没得过什么梦，能帮我押中一场马赛的赢家，或是预先瞧出股市要涨。」

===Original===
“Well, you know, there’s a case on record of a man who had a dream of hearing a radio narration of the English Derby of 1933, including the announcement that Hyperion had won, which he did,” Pierre said. “The dream was six hours before the race, and tallied very closely with the phraseology used by the radio narrator. Here.” He picked up a copy of Tyrrell’s *Science and Psychical Phenomena* and leafed through it.

===Chinese===
「这个嘛，你可知道，有案可稽的有这么一桩：有个人梦见自己听无线电播讲 1933 年的英国德比马赛，连『许佩里翁夺魁』的宣告都听见了——而这匹马后来果然赢了，」皮埃尔说，「梦做在开赛前六个钟头，同电台播音员实际用的措辞对得极近。给。」他拿起一册蒂勒尔的《科学与心灵现象》，翻检书页。

===Original===
“Did this fellow cash in on it?” Rand asked.

===Chinese===
「这位老兄可拿它换钱了？」兰德问。

===Original===
“No. He was a Quaker, and violently opposed to betting. Here.” He handed the book to Rand. “Case Twelve.”

===Chinese===
「没有。他是贵格会教徒，对打赌深恶痛绝。给。」他把书递给兰德。「第十二案。」

===Original===
Rand sat down on the edge of the desk, and read the section indicated, about three pages in length.

===Chinese===
兰德在桌沿坐下，读起指给他看的那一节，约莫三页。

===Original===
“Well, I’ll be damned!” he said, as he finished. The idea of anybody passing up a chance like that to enrich himself literally smote him to the vitals. “I see the British Society for Psychical Research checked that case, and got verification from a couple of independent witnesses. If the S.P.R. vouches for a story, it must be the McCoy; they’re the toughest-minded gang of confirmed skeptics anywhere in Christendom. They take an attitude toward evidence that might be advantageously copied by most of the district attorneys I’ve met, the one in this county being no exception.﻿ ﻿… What’s this story you’re working on?”

===Chinese===
「嗬，真是活见鬼！」他读完说道。一想到有人竟白白放过这样一个肥了自己的机会，这念头简直戳中他的心窝。「我看见英国心灵研究学会（British Society for Psychical Research）核查过此案，还从两位各自独立的证人处取得了证实。心灵研究学会（S.P.R.）肯作保的事，必是如假包换的真货；他们是整个基督教世界里头脑最铁、最死心塌地的一伙怀疑派。他们对待证据的那副态度，我结识过的大多数地区检察官都不妨照抄一份，本县这一位也不例外。……你手上这篇写的又是什么？」

===Original===
“Oh, it’s based on Dunne’s precognition theories, plus a few ideas of my own, plus a theory of alternate lines of time-sequence for alternate probabilities,” Pierre said. “See, here’s the situation﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「哦，底子是邓恩的预知理论，添上我自己几个点子，再加一套理论：不同的概率各有各的时间序列支线，」皮埃尔说，「你瞧，情形是这样……」

===Original===
Half an hour later, they were still arguing about a multidimensional universe when Rand remembered Dave Ritter, who should be at the Rosemont Inn by now. He looked at his watch, saw that it was five forty-five, and inquired about a telephone.

===Chinese===
半个钟头之后，两人还在为一个多维宇宙争执不下，兰德才想起戴夫·里特——这会儿他该到罗斯蒙特旅馆了。他看看表，五点三刻，便问电话在哪儿。

===Original===
“Yes, of course; out here.” Pierre took him back to the parlor, where he dialed the Inn and inquired if a Mr. Ritter, from New Belfast, were registered there yet.

===Chinese===
「有，当然；就在这边。」皮埃尔领他回到客厅，兰德在那儿拨通旅馆，问一位新贝尔法斯特来的里特先生可曾登记入住。

===Original===
He was. A moment later he was speaking to Ritter.

===Chinese===
登记了。不多时，他已同里特通上话。

===Original===
“Jeff, for Gawdsake, don’t come here,” Ritter advised. “This place is six-deep with reporters; the bar sounds like the second act of *The Front Page*. Tony Ashe and Steve Drake from the *Dispatch* and *Express*; Harry Bentz, from the *Mercury*; Joe Rawlings, the AP man from Louisburg; Christ only knows who all. This damn thing’s going to turn into another Hall-Mills case! Look, meet me at that beer joint, about two miles on the New Belfast side of Rosemont, on Route 19; the white-with-red-trimmings place with the big Pabst sign out in front. I’ll try to get there without letting a couple of reporters hide in the luggage-trunk.”

===Chinese===
「杰夫，千万看在老天爷的份上，别上这儿来，」里特劝道，「这地方记者摞了六层深；酒吧里那动静，活脱是《头版》（The Front Page）第二幕。《电讯暨晚快报》的托尼·阿什和史蒂夫·德雷克；《水星报》的哈里·本茨；路易斯堡来的美联社记者乔·罗林斯；天晓得还有哪一路的能人。这破案子眼看要闹成第二桩霍尔—米尔斯案！这么着：到那家啤酒店碰头，出罗斯蒙特往新贝尔法斯特去约两英里，19 号公路上；红镶边的白房子，门口竖一面帕布斯特（Pabst）大招牌的便是。我尽量摸过去，别让一两个记者钻进行李厢里跟着。」

===Original===
“Okay; see you directly.”

===Chinese===
「成；回头见。」

===Original===
Rand hung up, spent the next few minutes breaking away from Pierre and his mother, and went out to his car. Trust Dave Ritter, he thought, to pick some place where malt beverages were sold, for a rendezvous.

===Chinese===
兰德挂上电话，又花了几分钟才从皮埃尔和他母亲那里脱开身，出门上了车。他心想，戴夫·里特挑碰头的地点，果然离不开卖麦芽酒的地方。

===Original===
Dave’s coupé was parked inconspicuously beside the red-trimmed roadhouse. Opening his glove-box, Rand took out the two percussion revolvers and shoved them under his trench coat, one on either side, pulling up the belt to hold them in place. As he went into the roadhouse, he felt like Damon Runyon’s Twelve-Gun Tweeney. He found Ritter in the last booth, engaged in finishing a bottle of beer. Rand ordered Bourbon and plain water, and Ritter ordered another beer.

===Chinese===
戴夫那辆双门轿车不显山不露水地停在那座红镶白墙的路边酒店旁。兰德打开杂物箱，取出那两支火帽击发转轮枪，塞进风衣底下，一左一右各一支，再勒紧腰带把它们别稳。走进酒店时，他自觉活像达蒙·鲁尼恩笔下的「十二枪特威尼」。他在最末一个卡座里找到里特，那人正在收一瓶啤酒的尾。兰德要了波本威士忌外带一杯清水，里特又添了一瓶啤酒。

===Original===
“I have the stuff Tip left with Kathie,” Ritter said, taking out a couple of closely typed sheets and handing them across the table. “He said this was the whole business.”

===Chinese===
「蒂普留在凯蒂手上的东西，我带来了，」里特说着，掏出两页打得密密满满的纸，隔着桌子递过来。「他说，全部底细都在这上头了。」

===Original===
Rand glanced over them. Tipton had neatly and concisely summarized the provisions of Lane Fleming’s will, and had also listed all Fleming’s life insurance policies, with beneficiaries, including a partnership policy on the lives of Fleming, Dunmore, and Anton Varcek, paying each of the survivors $25,000.

===Chinese===
兰德大致过了一遍。蒂普顿把莱恩·弗莱明遗嘱的各项条文概述得干净利落、要言不烦，又列全了弗莱明的人寿保险单，附明受益人；其中包括一份以弗莱明、邓莫尔和安东·瓦尔切克三人生命合保的合伙保险单，幸存的合伙人每人可领两万五千美元。

===Original===
“I see Gladys and Geraldine and Nelda each get a third of Fleming’s Premix stock,” Rand commented. “But before they can have the certificates transferred to them, they have to sign over their voting-power to the board of directors. Evidently Fleming didn’t approve of the feminine touch in business.”

===Chinese===
「我看见格拉迪斯、杰拉尔丁和内尔达各得弗莱明那份普雷米克斯股票的三分之一，」兰德评道，「可轮到股票过户之前，得先把投票权签字让渡给董事会。显然，弗莱明是不赞成女人插手生意的。」

===Original===
“Yeah, isn’t that a dandy?” Ritter asked. “The directors are elected by majority vote of the stockholders. They now have the voting-power of a majority of the stock; that makes the present board self-perpetuating, and responsible only to each other.”

===Chinese===
「可不，这手设计难道不漂亮？」里特说，「董事由股东多数票选出。他们如今握有过半股票的投票权；这么一来，现任董事会便可以自行延续、万年不动，只对自己人负责。」

===Original===
“So it does, but that wasn’t what I was thinking of. According to Tip, the board is one hundred percent in favor of the merger with National Milling & Packaging. We’ll have to suppose Fleming knew that; there must have been considerable intramural acrimony on the subject while he was still alive. Now, since he opposed the merger, if he had intended committing suicide, he would have made some other arrangement, wouldn’t he? At least, one would suppose so. Well, then,” Rand asked, “why, since he is so worried about these suicide rumors, doesn’t Goode use the one argument which would utterly disprove them? Or is there some reason why he doesn’t want to call attention to the fact that Fleming’s death is what makes the merger possible?”

===Chinese===
「倒也真是万年不动，可我方才琢磨的不是这一层。据蒂普讲，董事会百分之百赞成同国民碾磨包装公司合并。这一点只得假定弗莱明生前心中有数；他在世时，公司内部为这事想必起过不小的火气。那么，既然他反对合并，他若真有意自杀，总该另行安排一番才对，是不是？至少，常情该作此想。那么好，」兰德问，「古德既然这么着急那些自杀的流言，为什么不用那条足以把流言斩草除根的论据呢？还是说，其中另有缘故——他不愿叫人注意到，正是弗莱明之死，才使这桩合并成为可能？」

===Original===
“Well, that would be calling attention to the fact that the merger made Fleming’s death necessary,” Ritter pointed out. He poured more beer into his glass. “While we’re on it, what’s the angle on this butler’s livery I was supposed to bring? I brought my tux, and I borrowed a striped vest from the Theatrical Property Exchange, and I brought that Dago .380 of yours. But what makes you think the Flemings are going to be needing a new butler? You going to poison the one they have?”

===Chinese===
「那个嘛，那岂不是叫人注意到，是合并使弗莱明的死成了必要，」里特点破道。他往杯里又斟了些啤酒。「既然说到这上头，我该捎的那身管家号衣，又是什么路数？无尾礼服我捎来了，条纹马甲是从戏剧道具调剂行（Theatrical Property Exchange）借的，你那支意大利佬 .380 我也捎来了。可你凭什么断定弗莱明家会缺个新管家？你打算把眼下这位药死？」

===Original===
“The one they have has been exceeding his duties,” Rand said. “He was supposed to clean the pistol-collection. Not content with that, he’s been cleaning it out. I know it was the butler.” He went, at length, into his reasons for thinking so, and described the modus operandi of the thefts. “Now, all this is just theory, so far, but when I’m able to prove it, I’m going to put the arm on this Walters, if it’s right in the middle of dinner and he only has the roast half served. And I want you ready to step into the vacancy thus created. I’m going to be busy as a pup in a fireplug factory with this Rivers thing, and I’ll need some checking-upping done inside the Fleming household.”

===Chinese===
「他们家眼下这位，早越出本分办差了，」兰德说，「派给他的活计，本是给那批手枪收藏清理保养。他倒不以此为足，索性把它『清』了个精光。我心里有数，正是这位管家。」接着，他把如此认定的种种缘由细细讲了一遍，又描述了那套偷天换日的作案手法（modus operandi）。「这些眼下还只是理论；可等我能坐实了，我就要下手拿这个沃尔特斯——哪怕正赶上晚宴吃到一半、烤肉才上得半道。我要你随时顶上由此腾出的空缺。里弗斯这摊子事够我忙的，忙得像跑进消防栓工厂的小狗；弗莱明宅子里里外外的查访，我还得靠你。」

===Original===
He went on, in meticulous detail, to explain about the Rivers murder. “I’ll have some work for you, before you’re ready to start buttling, too.” Disencumbering himself of the two percussion revolvers, he laid them on the table. “I want you to take these and show them to this barbecue man. Get from him a positive statement, preferably in writing, as to which, if either, he sold to Lane Fleming. You might show your Agency card and claim to be checking up on some stolen pistols that have been recovered. Then, if he identifies the Leech & Rigdon, take the Colt and show it to Elmer Umholtz. You want to be careful how you handle him; we may want him for puncturing Rivers, though I’m inclined to doubt that, as of now. Get him to tell you, yes or no, whether he reblued it and replated the back-strap and trigger-guard, and if he did it for Rivers; and if so, when. I know that’s been done; the bluing is too dark for a Civil War period job; the frame, which ought to be case-hardened in colors, has been blued like the barrel and cylinder, the cylinder-engraving is almost obliterated, and you can see a few rust-pits that have been blued over. But I want to know if this gun was ever in Rivers’s shop; that’s the important thing.”

===Chinese===
他又把里弗斯命案的方方面面，原原本本、巨细靡遗地讲了一遍。「等你准备走马上任当管家之前，也有活儿先派给你。」他卸脱身上那两支火帽击发转轮枪，搁上桌面。「我要你带上这两支，去给那个开烧烤摊的家伙过目。要从他嘴里取得一句确凿的话，最好落在纸面：这两支里不论哪一支，他到底卖没卖给过莱恩·弗莱明。你不妨亮出侦探社的证件，就说是核查一批已经追回的失窃手枪。随后，他若认下那支利奇与里格登，你就带上柯尔特去找埃尔默·乌姆霍尔茨。此人要留神对付；往后再要拿他问戳穿里弗斯这一案的罪，也说不定——尽管照眼前看，我倾向于不是他。要让他给你句准话，有，还是没有：他给这支枪重上过烤蓝没有、握把背板和扳机护圈重镀过银没有；干过的话，是不是替里弗斯干的；若是，又是什么时候。这些手脚，我知道都动过：那层烤蓝颜色太深，不是南北战争年代的活计；机匣本该烧成五彩淬火硬化的，却同枪管、弹轮一样上了蓝；弹轮上的雕纹几乎磨蚀干净，还看得出几处锈坑是拿烤蓝盖过去的。可我要弄明白的是，这支枪到底进没进过里弗斯的店——那才是顶要紧的。」

===Original===
“Uh-huh. Got the addresses?”

===Chinese===
「嗯哼。地址有了没有？」

===Original===
Rand furnished them, and Ritter noted them down. The waitress wandered back to see if they wanted anything else; she gave a small squeak of surprise when she saw the two big six-shooters on the table. Rand and Ritter repeated their orders, and when she brought back the drinks, the Colt and the Leech & Rigdon were out of sight.

===Chinese===
兰德一一报来，里特记下。女招待踅回来张罗，看两位还要些什么；一眼望见桌上那两支大号六响枪，她轻轻「呀」了一声。兰德和里特照原样又点了一遍；等她把酒端回来，柯尔特和利奇与里格登都已收得没了踪影。

===Original===
“The way I see it, everybody who’s within a light-year of this Rivers killing is trying to pin the medal on somebody else,” Ritter was saying. “The Lawrence girl was afraid young Jarrett had done it; right away, she sicced you onto Gillis. Gillis didn’t lose any time putting McKenna and Farnsworth onto Gresham. Gresham’s the only one who didn’t have a patsy ready; you’re supposed to dig one up for him. And Jarrett, the first chance he gets, introduces Umholtz.” He stared into his beer, as though he thought Ultimate Verity might be lurking somewhere under the suds. “Do you think it might be possible that Rivers bumped Fleming off, in spite of his getting killed later?” he asked.

===Chinese===
「依我看，凡是同这桩里弗斯命案沾得上一光年边儿的，个个都在想法把这枚奖章往别人胸口别，」里特正说着。「劳伦斯家那姑娘怕的是小贾勒特动的手；一转眼，她就把你往吉利斯身上一指。吉利斯也不含糊，立马把麦肯纳和法恩斯沃思引到格雷沙姆身上。格雷沙姆是独一份没备下替罪羊的；人家指望你给他现挖一个出来。贾勒特呢，一得着空子，就把乌姆霍尔茨端了出来。」他直勾勾盯着杯中的啤酒，仿佛认定「终极真理」正潜伏在泡沫底下某处。「你说，会不会有这么一种可能：里弗斯后来虽然送了命，弗莱明到底还是他干掉的？」他问。

===Original===
“Anything’s possible,” Rand replied, “except where some structural contradiction is involved, like scoring thirteen with one throw of a pair of dice. Yes, he could have. The way the Flemings leave their garage open as long as any of the cars are out, anybody could have sneaked into the house from the garage, and gone up from the library to the gunroom. The only question in my mind is whether Rivers would have known about that. That lawsuit and criminal action that Fleming was going to start﻿—and that’s been verified from sources independent of Goode﻿—was a good sound motive. And say he took the Leech & Rigdon away, after leaving the Colt in Fleming’s hand; selling it to some collector who’d put it in with a hundred or so other pistols would be a good way of disposing of it. And I can understand his trying to buy the Colt, to get it out of circulation.” Rand sipped his Bourbon. “But that leaves us with the question of who killed Rivers, and why.”

===Chinese===
「什么事都可能，」兰德答道，「只除了一种情形：牵涉到结构性的自相矛盾，好比一对骰子一把掷出十三点。是的，他干得成。弗莱明家有个习惯，车只要还有一辆在外头，车库便敞着不落；谁都能从车库溜进宅子，再从书房上到枪房。我心里唯一的疑问是，这一层里弗斯可曾知晓。弗莱明打算发难的那桩民事诉讼加刑事控告——此事已从古德以外的独立来源得到核实——是个扎实过硬的动机。再假定他把柯尔特塞进弗莱明手里之后，把利奇与里格登带走了；转卖给哪位收藏家、让它混进百十来支别的手枪堆里，倒是个处置的好法子。他设法把柯尔特买回来、好让它不再流通，我也想得通。」兰德啜了口波本。「可这么一来，剩下的问题还是：谁杀了里弗斯，又为什么。」

===Original===
“Well, because Fleming is dead﻿—and it doesn’t matter whether he was murdered or died of old age﻿—Walters starts robbing the collection. He sells the pistols to Rivers,” Ritter reconstructed. “And, as Rivers doesn’t want them around his shop till they’ve had time to cool off, he stores them with this Umholtz character, who seems to have been in plenty of crooked deals with Rivers in the past. The pistols are worth about ten grand, and nobody knows where they are but Rivers and Umholtz, and if Rivers drops dead all of a sudden, nobody will know where they are except Umholtz, and in a couple of years he can get them sold off and have the money all to himself.”

===Chinese===
「这么看：弗莱明死了——不论他是遭了凶手的，还是老死的，都无关紧要——沃尔特斯便下手掏那批收藏。他把手枪卖给里弗斯，」里特有条不紊地复原道，「里弗斯不想这批东西风头未过就在店里露脸，便把它们存到乌姆霍尔茨这个角色那里；此人同里弗斯合伙干的龌龊买卖，看来早不是一回两回。手枪约值一万块；藏身之处，天底下除里弗斯和乌姆霍尔茨再无人知。里弗斯若是突然暴毙，知道下落的便只剩乌姆霍尔茨一个；过上两年，他把东西陆续出脱，钱便全归他独吞了。」

===Original===
“Yes, Dave; that’s good sound murder, too. And Rivers would sit down and drink with Umholtz, and Umholtz could take that Mauser out of the rack right in front of Rivers and Rivers wouldn’t suspect a thing till it was too late. Of course, it depends upon two unverified assumptions: One, that the pistols were sold to Rivers, and, two, that Rivers stored them with Umholtz.”

===Chinese===
「对，戴夫；这也是一场扎扎实实的谋杀。何况里弗斯是肯坐下来同乌姆霍尔茨对饮的；乌姆霍尔茨尽可当着里弗斯的面，从枪架上取下那支毛瑟，等里弗斯起了疑心，早已迟了。当然，这套推演立在两个未经证实的假设上：其一，手枪确曾卖给里弗斯；其二，里弗斯确曾把它们存放在乌姆霍尔茨处。」

===Original===
“And, three, that Walters stole the pistols in the first place,” Ritter added. “You know, it’s possible that somebody else in that house might have stolen them.”

===Chinese===
「还有其三：手枪起初原是沃尔特斯偷的，」里特补上一句，「你要知道，那宅子里换了旁的什么人下手偷，也未可知。」

===Original===
“Yes. As I said, anything’s possible, within structural limits, but possibilities exist on different orders of probability. We can’t try to consider all the possibilities in any case, because they are indefinitely numerous; the best we can do is screen out all the low-order probabilities, list the high-order probabilities, and revise our list when and as new data comes to light. Well, I’ve told you why I think Walters is a good suspect. From what I’ve seen of that household, I think Walters was personally loyal to Lane Fleming, and I don’t believe he feels any loyalty to anybody else there, with the exception of Gladys Fleming. He might keep quiet about the missing pistols if she were the thief; if Dunmore, or Varcek, or either of the girls had done the stealing, he’d tell Gladys, and she’d pass it on to me. She would be glad of anything that could be used against any of the others. And if, on the other hand, she had stolen the pistols herself, she wouldn’t have wanted me poking around, and wouldn’t have brought me in, at least not to handle the collection.” Rand looked regretfully at his empty glass and decided against ordering another. “Dave, I just thought of something,” he said. “How do you think this would work?”

===Chinese===
「正是。我说过，在结构性限度之内，事事皆有可能；可可能性各按概率高低分属不同的阶。无论如何，我们没法把一切可能性都考虑周到，因为它们多得没有定数；我们至多能把低阶概率筛出去，把高阶概率列成单，再随新数据的冒头，随时修订这张单子。好了，我为什么把沃尔特斯看作一个够格的嫌疑对象，已经对你讲过。就我在这户人家所见，沃尔特斯对莱恩·弗莱明本人是尽忠的；除他之外，宅中任何人我都不信沃尔特斯肯为之尽忠——唯一的例外是格拉迪斯·弗莱明。偷枪的若是她，手枪失踪一节，他兴许会守口如瓶；若是邓莫尔、瓦尔切克，或是两位姑娘哪一位动的手，他便会告诉格拉迪斯，格拉迪斯自会递到我耳中。凡能拿去对付旁人的把柄，她都乐得抓。反过来，手枪若是她自己偷的，她便绝不肯让我上门嗅探，至少不肯让我经手那批收藏。」兰德怅然望了望空杯，打消了再要一杯的念头。「戴夫，我刚想起一件事，」他说，「你掂量掂量，这么办行不行？」

===Original===
He told Ritter what he had thought of. Ritter drank beer slowly and meditatively.

===Chinese===
他把心里盘算的事对里特讲了。里特慢慢呷着啤酒，边喝边寻思。

===Original===
“It just might work,” he considered. “I’ve seen that gag work a hundred times: hell, I’ve used something like that, myself, at least fifty times, and so have you. And I don’t think Walters would be familiar enough with dick-practice to see what you were doing. But if it turns out that Walters didn’t sell the pistols to Rivers at all, what then?”

===Chinese===
「没准儿真使得，」他掂量道，「这套把戏我见过一百回灵验：见鬼，差近的路数我自己也使过至少五十回，你也使过。况且沃尔特斯对侦探行当的门道，未必熟到瞧得出你在做什么。可万一到头来查明，沃尔特斯压根没把手枪卖给里弗斯，那又怎么着？」

===Original===
“Well, if he sold them to Umholtz, Pierre Jarrett’s theory is still valid until disproved,” Rand said. “And if he didn’t sell them either to Rivers or Umholtz, we’ll have to conclude that Rivers and Fleming were killed by the same person, the Rivers killing being a security measure. That is, unless we find that Rivers was killed by Pierre Jarrett, which is a sort of medium-high-order probability. Jarrett and the girl left Gresham’s early enough for him to have killed Rivers; they were both pretty hard hit by that twenty-five-grand blockbuster Rivers had dropped on them.﻿ ﻿… Give me back that Colt, Dave. All you have to do is get an identification on the Leech & Rigdon from the barbecue man. I’m going to let Mick McKenna handle Umholtz, one way or another, after we’ve concluded the Walters experiment. Until then, we don’t want to stir Umholtz up, at all.”

===Chinese===
「这么办：他若是卖给了乌姆霍尔茨，皮埃尔·贾勒特那套理论在被推翻之前便一直成立，」兰德说，「他若是里弗斯、乌姆霍尔茨两头都没卖，我们便只好认定，里弗斯与弗莱明死于同一人之手，杀里弗斯不过是一步灭口保安的棋。当然——除非我们查出里弗斯是皮埃尔·贾勒特杀的，那算得上一档中等偏高的概率。贾勒特同那位姑娘离开格雷沙姆家的时辰，早得尽够他动手杀了里弗斯；里弗斯甩在他们头上的那枚两万五千块的重磅炸弹，他们俩都挨得不轻。……把那支柯尔特还我，戴夫。你只消从烧烤摊那家伙嘴里，拿到对利奇与里格登的指认就成。沃尔特斯这场实验一收场，我就让米克·麦肯纳去收拾乌姆霍尔茨——不管走哪条路。在那之前，咱们一丝一毫不能惊动乌姆霍尔茨。」

## XV / 十五

===Original===
Parking in the drive, Rand entered the Fleming house by the front door. The butler must have been busy with his pre-dinner tasks in the rear; it was Gladys herself who admitted him.

===Chinese===
兰德把车停进车道，从前门进了弗莱明宅。管家想必正在后头忙晚饭前的各项活计；替他开门的正是格拉迪斯本人。

===Original===
"Stay out of there," she warned him, taking his arm and guiding him away from the parlor doorway. "Nelda and Geraldine are in there, ignoring each other. If you go in, they’ll start talking to you, and then they’ll start talking at each other through you, and the air will be full of tomahawks in a jiffy. Let’s go up in the gunroom; that’s out of the battle zone."

===Chinese===
「别上那边去，」她告诫他，挽住他的手臂，把他从客厅门口引开。「内尔达和杰拉尔丁在里头，正谁也不理谁。你一进去，她们就会先来跟你搭话，接着便隔着你冲对方放话，转眼间满屋子就得飞满战斧。咱们上枪房去吧；那儿在战线之外。」

===Original===
"What started the hostilities this time?" Rand asked, going up the stairway with her.

===Chinese===
「这回又是什么点燃了战火？」兰德问，随她一道上楼。

===Original===
"Oh, Geraldine lost Nelda’s place-marker out of the Kinsey Report, or something." She shrugged. "Mainly reaction to Rivers’s death. That was a great blow to all of us; twenty-five thousand dollars’ worth of blow. It was a blow to me, too, but I’m not letting it throw me.﻿ … What were you doing all afternoon?"

===Chinese===
「哦，杰拉尔丁把内尔达夹在《金赛报告》里的书签弄丢了，还是怎么的。」她耸耸肩。「主要是里弗斯之死引出的反应。那对我们大家都是一记狠棒；足足两万五千块分量的一记。对我也照样是当头一棒，可我不会让它击垮我。……你一下午都在干什么？」

===Original===
"Trying to keep the rest of our prospects out of jail. This sixteenth-witted District Attorney you have in this county had the idea he could charge Stephen Gresham with the killing. I had a time talking him out of it, and I’m still not sure how far I succeeded. And I was trying to get a line on where those pistols got to."

===Chinese===
「想法子别让我们其余那几位主顾蹲大牢。你们县里这位心眼只剩十六分之一的地区检察官，起了个念头，要拿这条人命给斯蒂芬·格雷沙姆定罪。我费了老大劲才劝住他，到现在也不敢说劝成了几分。另外我还想摸条线，看看那批手枪到底落到哪儿去了。」

===Original===
"Ssssh!" They reached the top of the stairs, and Rand saw Walters approaching down the hall. "It was Colonel Rand, Walters; I let him in myself. Are Mr. Varcek and Mr. Dunmore here, yet?"

===Chinese===
「嘘！」两人到了楼梯顶，兰德望见沃尔特斯正沿走廊迎面走来。「是兰德上校，沃尔特斯；我亲自放他进来的。瓦尔切克先生和邓莫尔先生到了没有？」

===Original===
"Mr. Dunmore is in the library, ma’am, and Mr. Varcek is upstairs, in his laboratory. Dinner will be ready in three-quarters of an hour."

===Chinese===
「邓莫尔先生在书房，太太；瓦尔切克先生在楼上他的实验室里。晚餐三刻钟后便好。」

===Original===
"Have you mixed the cocktails? You’d better do that. Serve them in about twenty minutes. And you’d better go up and warn Mr. Varcek not to become involved in anything messy before dinner."

===Chinese===
「鸡尾酒兑好了没有？你最好这就去办。约莫二十分钟后端上来。还有，你最好上楼知会瓦尔切克先生一声，别在晚饭前搅进什么脱不了手的麻烦里。」

===Original===
Walters yes-ma’am’d her and started toward the attic stairway. Rand and Gladys went into the gunroom; Rand turned to the left, picked a pistol from the wall, and carried it with him as he guided Gladys toward the desk in the corner.

===Chinese===
沃尔特斯连声应着「是，太太」，便朝通阁楼的楼梯走去。兰德和格拉迪斯进了枪房；兰德向左一转，从墙上摘下一支手枪，带着它引格拉迪斯走向角落里的书桌。

===Original===
"You think Walters stole them?" she asked.

===Chinese===
「你认为手枪是沃尔特斯偷的？」她问。

===Original===
"So far, I’m inclined to. Have you told any of the others, yet?"

===Chinese===
「就目前而言，我倾向于这么看。其余的人，你透露过没有？」

===Original===
"Oh, Lord, no! They’d all be sure that I stole them myself. I’m counting on you to get them back with as little fuss as possible. Do you think that was why Rivers was killed? After all, when a lot of valuable pistols disappear, and a crooked dealer is murdered, I’d expect there to be a connection."

===Chinese===
「哎呀，老天，可没有！他们保准全会认定是我自己偷的。我全指望你把它们尽可能悄没声儿地弄回来。你看里弗斯是不是就为这个送命的？毕竟，一大批值钱手枪失踪，一个奸诈的商人又遭了凶，总该有点干系才是。」

===Original===
"There could be. Did you ever hear any stories about Mrs. Rivers and this young fellow Gillis who works in Rivers’s shop?"

===Chinese===
「可能有干系。里弗斯太太同店里干活的那位吉利斯小伙子，你听到过些什么闲话没有？」

===Original===
Gladys laughed. "Is that rearing its ugly head in public, now?" she asked. "Well, there’s nothing like a good murder to shake the skeletons out of the closets. Not that this particular skeleton was ever exactly hidden. The stories are numerous, and somewhat repetitious; Cecil and Mrs. Rivers would be seen together, at roadhouses and so on, at what they imagined was a safe distance from Rosemont, and it was said that when Rivers was away over night, Cecil was never seen to leave the Rivers place in the evenings. Might this be relevant to Rivers’s sudden demise?"

===Chinese===
格拉迪斯笑了。「那桩丑事如今也公开冒头了？」她问。「唉，再没有一桩像样的命案，更能把柜子里的骷髅摇出声响来的了。倒也不是说这具骷髅藏得有多严实。传闻为数众多，而且大同小异：有人瞧见塞西尔同里弗斯太太出双入对，在路边酒店之类的地方，自以为离罗斯蒙特够远、够安全；还有人说，凡是里弗斯外宿未归的夜晚，从没人在晚间见塞西尔离开过里弗斯的店。这同里弗斯的暴毙，可有些相干么？」

===Original===
"It could be." Rand was keeping one eye on the hall door and the other on the head of the spiral stairway. "Don’t mention outside what I told you about Farnsworth having this brainstorm about Stephen Gresham. If it got out, it might hurt Gresham professionally. The fact is, Gresham has just retained me to investigate the Rivers murder for him. That won’t interfere to any great extent with the work I’m doing here; if necessary, I’ll bring a couple of my men in from New Belfast to help me on the Rivers operation." He broke off abruptly, catching a movement at the head of the spiral, and lifted the pistol in his hand, as though showing it to Gladys. "See," he went on, "it has two hammers and two nipples, but only one barrel. It was loaded with two charges, one on top of the other; the bullet of the rear charge acted as the breech-plug for the front charge.﻿ … Oh, Walters!" He affected to catch sight of the butler for the first time. "Bring me that .36 Walch revolver, will you?"

===Chinese===
「可能有。」兰德一只眼睛盯着走廊门，另一只盯着旋梯口。「我同你讲过法恩斯沃思对斯蒂芬·格雷沙姆起的那个怪念头，出了这宅子切莫外传。真传出去，会坏了格雷沙姆的职业前程。实情是，格雷沙姆方才已聘请我替他调查里弗斯命案。这不会太妨碍我在这里办的事；必要的话，我从新贝尔法斯特调几个人来，帮着办里弗斯这摊。」他蓦地收住话头——旋梯口有了动静——随手举起手里的枪，仿佛正指给格拉迪斯看。「瞧，」他接着说，「两个击锤、两个火嘴，却只有一根枪管。装的是两份发射药，一前一后叠着；后一份的弹丸，正好给前一份当闭气的膛塞。……哦，沃尔特斯！」他装出这才瞧见管家的样子。「把那支 .36 口径的瓦尔奇转轮枪给我拿来，劳驾。」

===Original===
"Yes, sir." Walters, crossing the room, veered to the right and went to the middle wall, bringing a revolver over to the desk. It was a percussion weapon with an abnormally long cylinder. "The cocktails are served," he announced.

===Chinese===
「是，先生。」沃尔特斯穿过房间，向右一拐，走到中间那面墙前，取了一支转轮枪送到桌边。那是一支火帽击发武器，弹轮长得异乎寻常。「鸡尾酒已经端上去了，」他通报道。

===Original===
"We’ll be down in a moment; you can put these back where they belong when you find time," Rand told him. "Now, here," he said to Gladys. "This is the same idea, in a revolver. Six chambers, two charges in each. In theory, it was a good idea, but in actual practice﻿ …"

===Chinese===
「我们这就下去；这几支你有空时放回原处，」兰德告诉他。「喏，你看，」他对格拉迪斯说，「同样的点子，用在了转轮枪上。六个弹膛，每膛两份发射药。理论上，这是个好点子，可实际用起来……」

===Original===
Walters went out the hall door, presumably to call Varcek. Rand continued talking about the superposed-load principle, as used in the Lindsay pistol and the Walch revolver, until he was sure the butler was out of hearing. Gladys was looking at him in appreciative if slightly punch-drunk delight.

===Chinese===
沃尔特斯出了走廊门，想必是叫瓦尔切克去了。兰德继续大谈叠装发射药原理——林赛手枪和瓦尔奇转轮枪用的便是这一套——直谈到他确信管家已出了听觉所及的范围。格拉迪斯望着他，那份欢喜半是赞赏，半是被打得晕头转向。

===Original===
"I wondered why you brought that thing over here with you," she said. "Brother, was that a quick shift!﻿ … You’re really sure he’s the one?"

===Chinese===
「我正纳闷你干吗把那家伙随身带过来，」她说，「好家伙，这一手换得真快！……你当真拿准就是他？」

===Original===
"I’m not really sure of anything, except of my own existence and eventual extinction," Rand told her. "It pretty nearly has to be somebody inside this house. I don’t think anybody else here, yourself included, would know enough about arms to rob this collection as selectively as it has been robbed. Did you see what just happened, here? I asked him for one of the most uncommon arms here, and he went straight and got it. He knows this collection as well as your husband did, and I assume he knows values almost as well.﻿ … And, of course, there was a musket, too; Mr. Fleming didn’t collect long-arms, or he’d have had one. It embodied the same principle as the pistol. The legend is that this man Lindsay’s brother was a soldier; he was supposed to have been killed by Indians who drew the fire of the detail he was with and then charged them when their muskets were empty." Rand shrugged. "Actually, the superposed-load principle is ancient; there’s a sixteenth-century wheel lock pistol in the Metropolitan Museum, in New York, firing two shots from the same barrel."

===Chinese===
「我对什么都不敢说真拿得准，除了我自己的存在和最终的寂灭，」兰德告诉她，「但十有八九，必是这宅子里的什么人。这屋里旁的人——你也在内——我不信有谁对兵器懂得够多，能像这样有拣有择地掏这批收藏。方才这儿的情形你看见了？我向他要这里最稀见的兵器之一，他径直走去便取来了。他对这批收藏的熟稔，不亚于你丈夫；我料想他对行情价码也几乎同样在行。……对了，当年还有一支滑膛枪；弗莱明先生不收长枪，不然必会收上一支。那枪用的是同一原理。传说这位林赛的兄弟当过兵；据讲是死在印第安人手里——那些人先引得他所在的小队放枪，再趁他们滑膛枪打空时扑了上来。」兰德耸耸肩。「其实，叠装发射药原理古已有之；纽约大都会博物馆里就藏着一支十六世纪的转轮打火手枪，同一根枪管连发两响。」

===Original===
Varcek and the butler, who had entered by the hall door, went across the gunroom and down the spiral. Rand laid down the pistol and escorted Gladys after them.

===Chinese===
瓦尔切克和管家——两人是从走廊门进来的——横穿枪房，顺旋梯下去了。兰德放下手枪，护送格拉迪斯随他们下去。

===Original===
Dunmore and Geraldine were in the library when they went down. Geraldine, mildly potted, was reclining in a chair, sipping her drink. Dunmore was still radiating his synthetic cheerfulness.

===Chinese===
他们下楼时，邓莫尔和杰拉尔丁都在书房。杰拉尔丁带着几分酒意，歪在椅子里小口啜着杯中物。邓莫尔还在周身发散他那副人工合成的兴高采烈。

===Original===
"Get many of the pistols listed, Colonel?" he hailed Rand, with jovial condescension.

===Chinese===
「手枪列出多少来了，上校？」他喜洋洋地摆出居高临下的派头，招呼兰德。

===Original===
"No." Rand poured two cocktails, handing one to Gladys. "I went to Arnold Rivers’s place this morning, on a little unfinished business, and damn near tripped over Rivers’s corpse. I spent the rest of the day getting myself disinvolved from the ensuing uproar," he told Dunmore. "You heard about it, of course."

===Chinese===
「没列出多少。」兰德斟了两杯鸡尾酒，递一杯给格拉迪斯。「今天上午我到阿诺德·里弗斯店里去，了结一点没办完的事，差点叫里弗斯的尸首绊个跟头。余下的大半天，我都花在把自己从随之而起的那场喧嚣里摘出来，」他告诉邓莫尔，「你当然已经听说了。」

===Original===
"Yes, of course. Horrible business. I hope you didn’t get mixed up in it any more than you had to. After all, you’re working for us, and if the police knew that, we’d be bothered, too.﻿ … Look here, you don’t think some of these other people who were after the collection might have killed Rivers, to keep him from outbidding them?"

===Chinese===
「当然听说了。可怕的事情。但愿你没被搅进去多深。毕竟，你是替我们办事的，警察若知道了这层，我们也要跟着受搅扰。……我说，你不认为旁那些盯着这批收藏的人里头，兴许有谁杀了里弗斯，免得他抬价压过他们？」

===Original===
Nelda, entering from the hallway, caught the last part of that.

===Chinese===
内尔达从门厅进来，正好听见后半截。

===Original===
"Good God, Fred!" she shrieked at him. "Don’t say things like that! Maybe they did, but wait till they’ve bought the collection and paid for it, before you start accusing them!"

===Chinese===
「老天爷，弗雷德！」她冲他尖声嚷道，「这种话可说不得！也许真是他们干的，可你也得等他们把这批收藏买下、钱付清了，再开口指控人家呀！」

===Original===
"I’m not accusing anybody," Dunmore growled back at her. "I don’t know enough about it to make any accusations. All I’m saying is—"

===Chinese===
「我又没指控谁，」邓莫尔朝她低吼回去，「我知道得根本不够，指控不了什么。我不过是说——」

===Original===
"Well, don’t say it, then, if you don’t know what you’re talking about," his wife retorted.

===Chinese===
「那么，既然自己都不知道自己在说些什么，就别说出口，」他太太回敬道。

===Original===
In spite of this start, dinner passed in relative quiet. For the most part, they talked about the remaining chances of selling the collection, about which nobody was optimistic. Rand tried to build up morale with pictures of large museums and important dealers, all fairly slavering to get their fangs into the Fleming collection, but to little avail. A pall of gloom had settled, and he was forced to concede that he had at last found somebody who had a valid reason to mourn the sudden and violent end of Arnold Rivers.

===Chinese===
虽有这一场开头，晚饭倒还算相对平静地过去了。席间大半在谈这批收藏脱手还剩几多指望，对此谁也不乐观。兰德搬出大博物馆和大经销商的图景来鼓舞士气——说它们个个垂涎三尺，急着把毒牙咬进弗莱明这批收藏——却收效甚微。一层愁云已然罩定，他只得承认：他总算找到了一个有正当理由哀悼阿诺德·里弗斯横死的人。

===Original===
Dinner finished, he went up to the gunroom and began compiling his list. He found a yardstick, and thumbtacked it to the edge of the desk to get overall and barrel lengths, and used a pair of inside calipers and a decimal-inch rule from the workbench to get calibers. Sticking a sheet of paper into the portable, he began on the wheel locks, leaving spaces to insert the description of the stolen pistols, when recovered. When he had finished the wheel locks, he began on the snaphaunces, then did the miguelet-locks. He had begun on the true flintlocks when Walters, who had finished his own dinner, came up to help him. Rand put the butler to work fetching pistols from the racks, and replacing those he had already listed. After a while, Dunmore strolled in.

===Chinese===
晚饭既毕，他上到枪房，着手编他的清册。他寻出一根码尺，用图钉钉在桌沿上，用来量全长和枪管长；又从工作台上取来一副内卡钳和一把十进制英寸尺，用来量口径。往手提打字机里卷进一页纸，他从转轮打火枪着手，留出空当，以便失窃手枪追回后补插说明。转轮打火枪录完，接着录撞簧枪，再录米格莱特枪机的。正录到真正的燧发枪时，自家晚饭也已用毕的沃尔特斯上来帮忙。兰德派给管家的活计，是从枪架上取枪，再把录完的放回原位。过了一阵，邓莫尔踱了进来。

===Original===
"You say you found Rivers’s body yourself, Colonel Rand?" he asked.

===Chinese===
「你是说，里弗斯的尸首是你亲手发现的，兰德上校？」他问。

===Original===
Rand nodded, finished what he was typing, and looked up.

===Chinese===
兰德点点头，把手头打的那行打完，抬起头来。

===Original===
"Why, yes. There were a few details I wanted to clear up with him, and I called at his shop this morning. I found him lying dead inside." He went on to describe the manner in which Rivers had met his death. "The radio and newspaper accounts were accurate enough, in the main; there were a few details omitted, at the request of the police, of course."

===Chinese===
「呃，正是。有几处细节我本想同他澄清，今早便去了他店里，发现他死在里头。」他接着把里弗斯毙命的情状描述了一遍。「电台和报纸的报道大体够准；有几处细节略去了，当然是应警方之请。」

===Original===
"Well, you didn’t get involved in it, though?" Dunmore inquired anxiously. "I mean, you’re not taking any part in the investigation? After all, we don’t want to be mixed up in anything like this."

===Chinese===
「那么，你总算没被搅进去吧？」邓莫尔焦急地打听，「我是说，调查你没插手吧？毕竟，这种事情我们可不想沾边。」

===Original===
"In that case, Mr. Dunmore, let me advise you not to discuss the matter of Rivers’s offer to buy this collection with anybody outside," Rand told him. "So far, the police and the District Attorney’s office both seem to think that Rivers was killed by somebody whom he’d swindled in a business deal. Of course, they know about the collection being for sale, and Rivers’s offering to buy it."

===Chinese===
「果真如此的话，邓莫尔先生，容我奉劝一句：里弗斯出价购买这批收藏一事，出了这宅子，休同任何人谈起，」兰德告诉他，「到眼下为止，警方同地区检察官办公室似乎都认为，里弗斯是被他在生意上坑过的人杀的。收藏出售、里弗斯有意购买，他们自然已经知道。」

===Original===
"They do?" Dunmore asked sharply. "Did you tell them that?"

===Chinese===
「他们知道？」邓莫尔尖声问，「这是你告诉的？」

===Original===
"Naturally. I had to account for my presence at Rivers’s shop, this morning," Rand replied. "I don’t know if the idea has occurred to them that somebody might have killed Rivers to eliminate a rival bidder for the collection or not; I wouldn’t say anything, if I were you, that might give them the idea."

===Chinese===
「自然是。今早我在里弗斯店里，总得有个说法，」兰德答道，「有没有人可能为了除掉这批收藏的竞价对手而杀里弗斯——这个念头他们转没转过，我不知道；若我是你，凡可能给他们递上这个点子的话，一句也不说。」

===Original===
The extension phone rang shrilly. Walters picked it up, spoke into it, and listened for a moment.

===Chinese===
分机电话铃声大作。沃尔特斯拿起听筒，对着它说了句什么，又听了一阵。

===Original===
"Yes, Miss Lawrence; he’s right here. You wish to speak to him?" He handed the phone across the desk to Rand. "Miss Karen Lawrence, for you, Colonel Rand."

===Chinese===
「是，劳伦斯小姐；他就在这儿。您要同他讲话？」他把电话隔着桌子递给兰德。「卡伦·劳伦斯小姐找您，兰德上校。」

===Original===
Rand took the phone. Before he had time to say "hello," the antique-shop girl demanded of him:

===Chinese===
兰德接过电话。「喂」字还没来得及出口，古董店那位姑娘已经劈头质问：

===Original===
"Colonel Rand, you must tell me the truth. Did you have anything to do with Pierre Jarrett’s being arrested?"

===Chinese===
「兰德上校，你必须对我说实话。皮埃尔·贾勒特被捕，你有没有份？」

===Original===
"*What?*" Rand barked. Then he softened his voice. "No; on my honor, Miss Lawrence. I knew nothing about it until this moment. Who did it? Olsen?"

===Chinese===
「什么？」兰德厉声喝问，随即放软了嗓音。「没有；我以名誉担保，劳伦斯小姐。直到方才这一刻，我对此一无所知。是谁干的？奥尔森？」

===Original===
"I don’t know what his name was. He was a State Police sergeant," she replied. "He and another State Policeman came to the Jarrett house about half an hour ago, charged Pierre with the murder of Arnold Rivers, and took him away. His mother phoned me about it a few minutes ago."

===Chinese===
「名字我不知道。是个州警中士，」她答道，「他同另一名州警约莫半点钟前到了贾勒特家，以谋杀阿诺德·里弗斯的罪名指控皮埃尔，把他带走了。几分钟前，他母亲打电话告诉了我。」

===Original===
"That Goddamned two-faced Jesuitical bastard!" Rand exploded. "Where are you now?"

===Chinese===
「那个天杀的两面三刀、耶稣会式的奸诈胚子！」兰德勃然大怒，「你这会儿在哪儿？」

===Original===
"Here at my shop. Mrs. Jarrett is coming here. She’s afraid the reporters will be coming out to the house as soon as they hear about it, and she doesn’t want to talk to them."

===Chinese===
「在我店里。贾勒特太太正往这边来。她怕记者一得着风声就会涌到宅子去，她不想同他们打交道。」

===Original===
"All right. I’ll be there as soon as I can. If there’s anything I can do to help you, you can count on me for it."

===Chinese===
「好。我尽快赶到。凡有我能帮上忙的地方，只管指望我。」

===Original===
He hung up, and turned to Walters. "Is my car still out front?" he asked. "It is? Good. I’ll be gone for a while; tell the others I have something to attend to."

===Chinese===
他挂上电话，转向沃尔特斯。「我的车还在前头？」他问，「在？好。我要出去一阵子；告诉其余的人，我有点事要办。」

===Original===
"What’s happened now?" Dunmore asked sourly.

===Chinese===
「这回又出了什么事？」邓莫尔没好气地问。

===Original===
"Just what I was speaking about. The Gestapo gathered up Pierre Jarrett; they seem to have gotten the idea, now, that the motive may have been competition for the collection. Next thing, Farnsworth will think he has a case against Carl Gwinnett, and he’ll land in the jug, too. I hope you realize that every time something like this happens, it peels a thousand or so off the price I’ll be able to get for you people for these pistols."

===Chinese===
「正是我方才说的事。盖世太保把皮埃尔·贾勒特收走了；他们如今似乎转过了念头，认定动机或许是争夺这批收藏。下一步，法恩斯沃思该觉得他手里有了告卡尔·格温内特的案由，格温内特也要跟着蹲号子。我希望你们明白：这种事每出一桩，我能替你们这帮人卖这批手枪的价钱，就得给剥掉一千来块。」

===Original===
Dunmore didn’t try to ask how that would happen, for which Rand was duly thankful; he accepted the statement uncritically. Walters was staring at Rand in horror, saying nothing. Rand picked up the outside phone and dialed the same number he had called from the Rivers place that morning.

===Chinese===
邓莫尔没追问这话怎么讲，兰德对此正当感激；他全盘接受了这个说法，不置一词疑问。沃尔特斯满眼惊恐地瞪着兰德，一言不发。兰德拿起外线电话，拨的正是那天早晨他从里弗斯店里拨过的同一个号码。

===Original===
"Is Sergeant McKenna about?﻿ … He is? Fine; I’d like to speak to him.﻿ … Oh, hello, Mick; Jeff Rand."

===Chinese===
「麦肯纳中士在么？……在？好；我找他讲话。……哦，你好，米克；杰夫·兰德。」

===Original===
McKenna chuckled out of the receiver. "Sort of slipped one over on you, didn’t I?" he gloated. "Why, I was checking up on those people who were at Gresham’s, last evening, and they all agreed that young Jarrett and the Lawrence girl had left the party about ten. So I had a talk with Miss Lawrence, and she tried to tell me that Jarrett was with her at her apartment, over the antique shop, from about ten fifteen until about twelve, when another girl she rooms with got home from a date. I’d of took that, too, only right across the street from the antique shop there is one of these old hens like you find in every neighborhood, the kind that keeps their nose flattened on the window between the curtains, checking up on the neighbors. I spotted her when I came out of the antique shop, so I slipped around to see her, and she told me that young Jarrett went into the apartment with the girl at about quarter past ten, stayed inside for about twenty minutes, then came out and drove away. She says Jarrett came back in about half an hour, and stayed till this girl who shares the Lawrence girl’s apartment﻿—a Miss Dupont, who teaches sixth grade at Thaddeus Stevens School﻿—got home, about twelve. So there you are."

===Chinese===
听筒里传来麦肯纳的轻笑。「多少算是从你眼皮底下溜过去了一手，是不是？」他扬扬得意。「是这样：我去查昨晚在格雷沙姆家的那拨人，他们众口一词，说小贾勒特同劳伦斯家那位姑娘十点上下就离了席。我便找劳伦斯小姐谈了谈，她一口咬定，从十点一刻到十二点上下，贾勒特一直同她在古董店楼上的公寓里，直到同她合住的那位姑娘幽会回来。换了我也认这个账——偏偏古董店马路正对面，就住着那种每个街区都找得着一只的老母鸡，成天把鼻子压扁在窗帘夹层的玻璃上，查着街坊四邻的岗。我从古董店出来时一眼瞟见了她，便绕过去拜访，她告诉我：小贾勒特十点一刻上下随那姑娘进了公寓，在里头待了约莫二十分钟，便出来开车走了。她说贾勒特约莫半个钟头后又折回来，一直待到同劳伦斯姑娘合住公寓的那位——一位杜邦小姐，在萨迪厄斯·史蒂文斯学校教六年级——十二点上下幽会回来。就是这么回事。」

===Original===
"Uh-huh. Dave Ritter said this was going to turn into another Hall-Mills case; well, now you have your Pig Woman," Rand said. "Miss Lawrence shouldn’t have lied to you, Mick. I suppose she got worried when you started asking questions, and there’s nothing like a good murder in the neighborhood to make liars out of people."

===Chinese===
「嗯哼。戴夫·里特说过，这案子要闹成第二桩霍尔—米尔斯案；瞧，如今你的『猪女人』也有了，」兰德说，「劳伦斯小姐不该对你撒谎，米克。我猜你一起头问话，她就慌了神；再说，左近再没有比一桩像样命案更能把人变成撒谎精的了。」

===Original===
"And damn well I know that!" McKenna agreed. "But that isn’t all. It seems our cruise-car crew spotted Jarrett’s car standing in Rivers’s drive, about eleven. Just when he was away from the antique-shop, and about when the M.E. figures Rivers was getting the business."

===Chinese===
「这个我他妈太清楚了！」麦肯纳附和，「可还没完。我们的巡逻车班组好像在十一点上下，瞧见贾勒特的车停在里弗斯家的车道上。恰是他离开古董店的当口，也恰是法医估算里弗斯挨那一记的时辰。」

===Original===
"Did they get the number?" Rand asked. "Or how did they identify the car?"

===Chinese===
「车牌号他们记下了没有？」兰德问，「不然，那车是怎么认出来的？」

===Original===
"Oh, they knew it; see, our boys shoot a lot with the Scott County Rifle & Pistol Club, and they’ve all seen Jarrett’s car at the range, different times," McKenna said. "A gray 1947 Plymouth coupé. Like I say, they knew the car, and they knew Jarrett collects guns, and the lights were on inside the shop and the shades were drawn, so they didn’t think anything of it, at the time. See, they went to bed about ten this morning, and didn’t get up till after five, so I didn’t find out about it till after supper."

===Chinese===
「嗐，认得那车；是这样，我们那帮小伙子常同斯科特县来复枪与手枪俱乐部一道打枪，靶场上谁都不同次数地见过贾勒特的车，」麦肯纳说，「灰色 1947 年普利茅斯双门轿车。我说了，车他们认得，贾勒特收藏枪械他们也知道，店里灯亮着、遮帘又放下了，所以当时谁也没多想。是这样，他们今早十点上下才睡下，五点过后才起身，我一直到晚饭后才得知这一茬。」

===Original===
Rand shrugged, and managed to get some of the shrug into his voice. "Can be, at that," he said. "I hope you’re not making a mistake, Mick; if you are, his lawyer’s going to crucify you. What are you using for a motive?"

===Chinese===
兰德耸了耸肩，还设法把这份耸肩揉进了嗓音里。「也真有这个可能，」他说，「我只指望你别是弄错了，米克；真弄错了，他的律师会把你钉上十字架。动机你们打算用哪一条？」

===Original===
"Rivers was outbidding this crowd Jarrett and the girl were in with. They all told me about that," McKenna said. "And he and the girl were planning to use their end of the collection to go into the arms business, after they got married. Rivers got in the way." McKenna, at the other end of the line, must have shrugged, too. "After all, for about four years, they’d been training Jarrett to overcome resistance with the bayonet, so he did just that."

===Chinese===
「里弗斯抬价，压过了贾勒特同那姑娘合伙的那伙人。这事他们全对我讲了，」麦肯纳说，「再说他同那姑娘正打算婚后拿他们名下那份收藏做本钱，进军军火生意。里弗斯挡了道。」电话另一头的麦肯纳，想必也耸了耸肩。「毕竟，四年上下，他们一直在训练贾勒特用刺刀克服抵抗，他可不就照办了。」

===Original===
"Maybe so. You find out anything about that other matter I was interested in?"

===Chinese===
「也许是这样。我关心的另一桩事，你查出什么没有？」

===Original===
"You mean the pistols? Huh-unh; we went over Rivers’s place with a fine-tooth comb, and questioned young Gillis about it, and we didn’t get a thing. You sure those pistols went to Rivers?"

===Chinese===
「你是说那批手枪？没有的事；我们把里弗斯的店拿细篦子篦了一遍，就这事也审了小吉利斯，一个子儿的线索也没捞着。你拿准那批手枪到了里弗斯手里？」

===Original===
"I’m not sure of anything at all," Rand replied, looking at his watch. "You going to be in, say in a couple of hours? I want to have a talk with you."

===Chinese===
「我对什么都谈不上拿准，」兰德答道，看了看表，「比如说，过两个钟头，你在不在？我要同你谈一谈。」

===Original===
"Sure. I’ll be around all evening," McKenna assured him. "If we don’t have another murder."

===Chinese===
「在。一晚上我都在，」麦肯纳向他保证，「只要别再来一桩命案。」

===Original===
Rand hung up. He pulled the sheet out of the typewriter, laid it face down on the other sheets he had finished, and laid a long seventeenth-century Flemish flintlock on top for a paperweight, memorizing the position of the pistol relative to the paper under it.

===Chinese===
兰德挂上电话。他把那页纸从打字机里抽出，面朝下覆在已完成的其余各页上，又取来一支长长的十七世纪弗拉芒燧发枪压在上面当镇纸，把枪与底下纸张的相对位置记进脑子里。

===Original===
"Put those pistols back on the wall," he told Walters, indicating several he had laid aside after listing. "Leave the others there; I’m not finished with them yet. I’ll be back before too long. If I don’t find any more bodies."

===Chinese===
「把那几支手枪放回墙上去，」他吩咐沃尔特斯，指的是录完后搁在一旁的那几支，「其余的留着不动；我还没弄完。我去去就回。只要别再撞上别的尸首。」

## XVI / 十六

===Original===
It was raining again as Rand parked his car about a hundred yards up the street from Karen Lawrence’s antique-shop. The windows were dark, but Karen was waiting inside the door for him. He entered quickly, mindful of the All-Seeing Eye across the street, and followed her to a back room, where Mrs. Jarrett and Dorothy Gresham were. All three women regarded him intently, as though trying to decide whether he was friend or enemy. There was a long silence before Mrs. Jarrett spoke, and when she did, her words were almost the same as Karen’s when she had spoken over the phone.

===Chinese===
兰德把车停在卡伦·劳伦斯那家古董店沿街过去约一百码处，天又下起雨来。店面橱窗黑着，卡伦却在门内候他。他心里惦着街对面那只无所不见的眼睛，赶紧进门，随她进了后屋——贾勒特太太和多萝西·格雷沙姆都在那里。三个女人都直勾勾地打量他，仿佛在断定他是友是敌。半晌没人说话，贾勒特太太才终于开口；而她的话，同卡伦方才在电话里讲的几乎一字不差。

===Original===
“Colonel Rand,” she began, obviously struggling with herself, “you must tell me the truth. Did you have anything to do with my son’s being arrested?”

===Chinese===
「兰德上校，」她开了口，显然在心里苦苦挣扎，「您必须对我讲实话。我儿子被捕这件事，您有没有份？」

===Original===
Rand shook his head. “Absolutely nothing, Mrs. Jarrett,” he told her, unbuckling the belt of his raincoat and taking it off. “I have never seriously suspected your son of the Rivers murder, I had no idea that McKenna was contemplating arresting him, and if I had, I would have advised him against it. Besides causing annoyance to innocent people, McKenna’s made a serious tactical error. He was misled by appearances, and he was afraid I’d break this case before he did, which I intend to do.” He turned to Karen Lawrence. “I talked to McKenna after you called me; he as much as admitted making that arrest to get in ahead of me.”

===Chinese===
兰德摇头。「半点也没有，贾勒特太太，」他边说边解下雨衣腰带，把雨衣脱下来。「里弗斯的案子，我从来没有认真疑心过令郎；麦肯纳打算抓他，我事先一无所知——若是知道，必会劝阻。麦肯纳这一手，除了平白搅扰无辜，还犯下一个严重的战术错误。他让表象牵着走了，又怕我抢在他前头破案——而这恰是我打算干的。」他转向卡伦·劳伦斯。「你来过电话后，我找麦肯纳谈了；他差不多等于承认，那一次逮捕，就是做来抢在我前面的。」

===Original===
“I told you,” Dorothy Gresham flashed at the others. “I knew Jeff wouldn’t stoop to anything as contemptible as pretending to be Pierre’s friend and then getting him arrested!”

===Chinese===
「我早说过吧！」多萝西·格雷沙姆冲另外两人抢白道。「我就知道杰夫绝不会下作到这个地步——装作皮埃尔的朋友，转头却害他落到被捕！」

===Original===
Rand permitted himself a wry inward smile. He hoped she would not have an opportunity to observe his stooping capabilities before he had finished his various operations at Rosemont.

===Chinese===
兰德由着自己在心里苦笑了一下。他只盼在罗斯蒙特的各项行动收场之前，她不至于有机会领教他那「下作」的本事。

===Original===
“I certainly hoped not.” Mrs. Jarrett relaxed, smiling faintly at Rand. “Pierre likes you, Colonel. I hated the thought that you might have betrayed him. Are you working on the Rivers case, too?”

===Chinese===
「我当然也巴不得不是。」贾勒特太太松了下来，朝兰德淡然一笑。「皮埃尔喜欢您，上校。想到您也许出卖过他，我心里就发苦。里弗斯那桩案子，您也在查吗？」

===Original===
Rand nodded again, turning to Dot Gresham. “Your father retained me to make an investigation,” he said. “After that trouble he had with Rivers about that spurious North & Cheney, he wanted the murderer caught before somebody got around to accusing him.”

===Chinese===
兰德又点了点头，转向多特·格雷沙姆。「你父亲聘了我做调查，」他说。「他同里弗斯为那支假冒的诺斯与切尼起了那场纠纷之后，就想赶紧捉住真凶，免得回头有人咬到他头上。」

===Original===
“You mean there’s a chance Dad might be suspected?” Dot was scared.

===Chinese===
「你是说，爸爸有可能受到怀疑？」多特慌了。

===Original===
Rand nodded. The girl was beginning to look suspiciously at Karen and Mrs. Jarrett. Getting ready to toss Pierre to the wolves if her father were in danger, Rand suspected. He hastened to reassure her.

===Chinese===
兰德点头。姑娘已开始拿怀疑的眼光打量卡伦和贾勒特太太。兰德猜，她是在做准备：父亲一旦有险，就把皮埃尔扔出去喂狼。他忙不迭宽她的心。

===Original===
“Rivers was still alive when your father reached home, last evening,” he told her. “That’s been established.”

===Chinese===
「昨晚你父亲到家的时候，里弗斯还活着，」他告诉她。「这一点已经查实。」

===Original===
She breathed her obvious relief. If Gresham had left home after Rand’s departure with Philip Cabot, she didn’t know it.

===Chinese===
她的如释重负，明明白白化作一声长呼。倘若格雷沙姆在兰德同菲利普·卡伯特走后还出过家门，至少她是不知道的。

===Original===
Karen, on the other hand, was growing more and more worried.

===Chinese===
卡伦这边，却是愁云愈聚愈浓。

===Original===
“Look, Colonel,” she began. “They didn’t just pull Pierre’s name out of a hat. They must have had something to suspect him about.”

===Chinese===
「听我说，上校，」她开了腔。「他们把皮埃尔的名字拎出来，总不是从帽子里凭空摸出来的。他们必定捏着点什么，才疑到他头上。」

===Original===
“Yes. You shouldn’t have lied to McKenna. He checked up on your story; the woman across the street told him about seeing Pierre leave here a little before eleven and come back about half an hour later.”

===Chinese===
「不错。你本不该对麦肯纳撒谎。他把你的话核对了一遍；街对面那个女人告诉他，说亲眼见皮埃尔十一点前不久离开这儿，约莫半个钟头后回来。」

===Original===
“I was afraid of that,” Karen said. “I forgot all about that old hag. There’s nothing that can go on around here that she doesn’t know about; Pierre calls her Mrs. G2.”

===Chinese===
「我就怕这个，」卡伦说。「我把那老巫婆忘得死死的。这一带不论出什么事，没有她不知道的；皮埃尔管她叫『G2 太太』。」

===Original===
“And then,” Rand continued, “McKenna claims that a car like Pierre’s was seen parked in Rivers’s drive about the time Pierre was away from here.”

===Chinese===
「还有，」兰德接着说，「麦肯纳声称，皮埃尔离开这里的当口，有人看见一辆同他的车相仿的汽车，停在里弗斯家的车道上。」

===Original===
Mrs. Jarrett moaned softly; her face, already haggard, became positively ghastly. Karen gasped in fright.

===Chinese===
贾勒特太太低低呻吟了一声；那张本就憔悴的脸，登时灰败得吓人。卡伦惊得倒抽一口冷气。

===Original===
“They only identified it as to model and make; they didn’t get the license number … Where did Pierre go, while he was away from here?”

===Chinese===
「他们只认了个车型厂牌；车牌号并没有拿到……皮埃尔离开这儿那阵子，到底去了哪里？」

===Original===
“He went out for cigarettes,” Karen said. “When we came here from Greshams’, we made some coffee, and then sat and talked for a while, and then we found out that we were both out of cigarettes and there weren’t any here. So Pierre said he’d go out and get some. He was gone about half an hour; when he came back, he had a carton, and some hot pork sandwiches. He’d gotten them at the same place as the cigarettes—Art Igoe’s lunch-stand.”

===Chinese===
「出去买烟了，」卡伦说。「我们从格雷沙姆家过来之后，煮了咖啡，坐着聊了一阵，后来发现两个人的烟都抽光了，屋里又一支也寻不出。皮埃尔就说他出去买点来。他走了约莫半个钟头，回来时带着一条烟，还有几份热腾腾的猪肉三明治。烟和三明治是在同一个地方买的——阿特·艾戈的小吃摊。」

===Original===
“Could Igoe verify that?”

===Chinese===
「艾戈能证实这一节吗？」

===Original===
“It wouldn’t help if he did. Igoe’s place isn’t a five-minute drive from Rivers’s, farther down the road.”

===Chinese===
「他证实了也帮不上忙。艾戈那个摊子，顺着大路再往前，离里弗斯的店开车用不了五分钟。」

===Original===
“Has Pierre a lawyer?” Rand asked.

===Chinese===
「皮埃尔请律师了没有？」兰德问。

===Original===
“No. Not yet. We were just talking about that.”

===Chinese===
「没有。还没有。我们正说这个呢。」

===Original===
“Dad would defend him,” Dot suggested. “Of course, he’s not a criminal lawyer—”

===Chinese===
「爸爸可以替他辩护呀，」多特出主意道。「当然，他不是办刑事案的律师——」

===Original===
“Carter Tipton, in New Belfast,” Rand told them. “He’s my lawyer; he’s gotten me out of more jams than you could shake a stick at. Where’s the telephone? I’ll call him now.”

===Chinese===
「新贝尔法斯特的卡特·蒂普顿，」兰德告诉她们。「他是我的律师；经他的手把我从麻烦里捞出来，捞的回数多到你们数不过来。电话在哪儿？我现在就打。」

===Original===
“You think he’d defend Pierre?”

===Chinese===
「你觉得他肯替皮埃尔辩护？」

===Original===
“Unless I’m badly mistaken, Pierre isn’t going to need any trial defense,” Rand told them. “He will need somebody to look after his interests, and we’ll try to get him out on a writ as soon as possible.”

===Chinese===
「除非我大错特错，皮埃尔是用不着什么出庭辩护的，」兰德告诉她们。「他需要的是有人照管他的权益；我们要设法尽早申请人身保护令，把他弄出来。」

===Original===
He looked at his watch. It was ten minutes to nine. It was hard to say where Carter Tipton would be at the moment; his manservant would probably know. Karen showed him the phone and he started to put through a person-to-person call.

===Chinese===
他看了看表。九点差十分。卡特·蒂普顿此刻人在哪里，很难说；他的贴身男仆多半知道。卡伦把电话指给他，他开始拨一通叫人电话。

---

===Original===
It was eleven o’clock before he backed his car into the Fleming garage, and the rain had turned to a wet, sticky snow. All the Fleming cars were in, but Rand left the garage doors open. He also left his hat and coat in the car.

===Chinese===
他把车倒进弗莱明家的车库时已是十一点，雨也转成了又湿又黏的雪。弗莱明家的汽车全数入库，兰德却让车库门敞着，帽子外衣也留在了车里。

===Original===
After locating and talking to Tipton and arranging for him to meet Dave Ritter at the Rosemont Inn, he had gone to the State Police substation, where he had talked at length with Mick McKenna. He had been compelled to tell the State Police sergeant a number of things he had intended keeping to himself. When he was through, McKenna went so far as to admit that he had been a trifle hasty in arresting Pierre Jarrett. Rand suspected that he was mentally kicking himself with hobnailed boots for his premature act. He also submitted, for McKenna’s approval, the scheme he had outlined to Dave Ritter, and obtained a promise of cooperation.

===Chinese===
他先找到蒂普顿、同他谈妥，安排他去罗斯蒙特旅馆与戴夫·里特碰头；随后去了州警分局，同米克·麦肯纳长谈了一场。他被迫向这位州警中士交了底，说出不少原本打算烂在自己肚子里的东西。等他讲完，麦肯纳甚至承认，逮捕皮埃尔·贾勒特一事，自己确是有些操之过急。兰德疑心他正在心里穿着钉靴，为自己的孟浪狠踹自己。他先前向戴夫·里特勾画过的那套方案，也呈给麦肯纳请予核准，并讨得了一句合作的许诺。

===Original===
When he entered the Fleming library, en route to the gunroom, he found the entire family assembled there; with them was Humphrey Goode. As he came in, they broke off what had evidently been an acrimonious dispute and gave him their undivided attention. Geraldine, relaxed in a chair, was smoking; for once, she didn’t have a glass in her hand. Gladys occupied another chair; she was smoking, too. Nelda had been pacing back and forth like a caged tiger; at Rand’s entrance, she turned to face him, and Rand wondered whether she thought he was Clyde Beatty or a side of beef. Goode and Dunmore sat together on the sofa, forming what looked like a bilateral offensive and defensive alliance, and Varcek, looking more than ever like Rudolf Hess, stood with folded arms in one corner.

===Chinese===
去枪房须打书房经过，兰德进门便发现全家都聚在那里；同他们在一起的还有汉弗莱·古德。他一进来，众人便打断了那场显然火药味十足的争执，把注意力齐刷刷全给了他。杰拉尔丁歪在一张椅子里抽烟；破天荒头一遭，手里没擎酒杯。格拉迪斯坐在另一张椅子上，也在抽烟。内尔达本来正像笼中猛虎似的来回踱步；兰德一进来，她霍地转身正对他——兰德倒纳闷，她把他当成了驯狮名家克莱德·贝蒂呢，还是一扇牛肉。古德同邓莫尔并肩坐在沙发上，结成了一副双边攻守同盟的架势；瓦尔切克抱着双臂站在一个角落里，那张脸比以往任何时候都更像鲁道夫·赫斯。

===Original===
“Now, see here, Rand,” Dunmore began, as soon as the detective was inside the room, “we want to know just exactly for whom you’re working, around here. And I demand to know where you’ve been since you left here this evening.”

===Chinese===
「喂，把话说清楚，兰德，」侦探刚进屋，邓莫尔便开了腔。「我们想知道，你在这儿转悠，到底是替谁办事。我还非要你说清楚不可：今晚从这儿出去以后，你上哪儿去了？」

===Original===
“And I,” Goode piped up, “must protest most strongly against your involvement in this local murder case. I am informed that, while in the employ of this family, you accepted a retainer from another party to investigate the death of Arnold Rivers.”

===Chinese===
「还有我，」古德尖起嗓子插话，「必须对你的介入提出最强烈的抗议——介入本地这桩凶案。据我所知，你受着这一家的雇用，却又收下另一方的聘金，去调查阿诺德·里弗斯的死。」

===Original===
“That’s correct,” Rand informed him. Then he turned to Gladys. “Just for the record, Mrs. Fleming, do you recall any stipulation to the effect that the business of handling this pistol-collection should have the exclusive attention of my agency? I certainly don’t recall anything of the sort.”

===Chinese===
「没错，」兰德答他，随即转向格拉迪斯。「不过把话说明白，弗莱明太太：您可记得有过什么条款，约定经办这批手枪收藏的事宜，须由敝社独家效力？反正我全然不记得有这回事。」

===Original===
“No, of course not,” she replied. “As long as the collection is sold to the best advantage, I haven’t any interest in any other business of your agency, and have no right to have.” She turned to the others. “I thought I made that clear to all of you.”

===Chinese===
「没有，当然没有，」她答道。「只要这批收藏能卖到最好的价钱，贵社别的业务我一概没有兴趣，也无权有兴趣。」她转向众人。「我以为这话早跟你们各位都交代清楚了。」

===Original===
“You didn’t answer my question!” Dunmore yelled at him.

===Chinese===
「你没有回答我的问题！」邓莫尔朝他吼道。

===Original===
“I don’t intend to. You aren’t my client, and I’m not answerable to you.”

===Chinese===
「我也不打算回答。你不是我的委托人，我不欠你交代。」

===Original===
“Well, you carry my authorization,” Goode supported him. “I think I have a right to know what’s being done.”

===Chinese===
「哼，你可带着我的授权呢，」古德帮腔道。「我想我有权知道你干的都是些什么。」

===Original===
“As far as the collection’s concerned, yes. As for the Rivers murder, or my armored-car service, or any other business of the Tri-State Agency, no.”

===Chinese===
「就收藏而言，可以。至于里弗斯命案，我的装甲运钞业务，或三州侦探社的任何其他业务——不可以。」

===Original===
“Well, you made use of my authorization to get that revolver from Kirchner—” Goode began.

===Chinese===
